# DOSYA: game-host/README.md
##############################################################################
# OYUN HOST (bağımsız oyun servisi)

Bu depo **Taskonomix'ten tamamen ayrıdır**. Kendi VPS'imizde, kendi Coolify
resource'umuzda, kendi domainimizde çalışır. Taskonomix kodu, veritabanı veya
deploy'u ile hiçbir bağlantısı yoktur.

- Veritabanı yok
- `/api` yok
- Telegram `initData` işlenmez
- SPA fallback yok; bilinmeyen yol = 404
- `/healthz` → `ok`

**Canlı oyunlar:**
- https://oyun.179.253.91.172.sslip.io/games/gem-match/
- https://oyun.179.253.91.172.sslip.io/games/word-hunt/

(`/healthz` → ok)

## Ayrı uygulamalar (önerilen)

Her oyunun kendi Coolify uygulaması ve kendi Dockerfile'ı var:

| Oyun | Dockerfile | Domain |
|---|---|---|
| Gem Match | `game-host/Dockerfile.gem-match` | `https://gemmatch.179.253.91.172.sslip.io/` |
| Kelime Avı | `game-host/Dockerfile.word-hunt` | `https://kelimeavi.179.253.91.172.sslip.io/` |

Build context yine repo kökü; `GAME_ID` env değeri sadece o oyunu kök dizinde servis eder.
Eski ortak `oyun` uygulaması kaldırıldı; her oyun kendi container'ında çalışır.
`Dockerfile` (ortak mod) yalnızca yerel geliştirme/yzdek amaçlı duruyor.

## Yerel test

```bash
npm install
npm run build:games
node game-host/server.cjs         # → http://localhost:3000/games/gem-match/
```

## Coolify kurulumu

1. Coolify → **New resource** → **Public Repository** → `tfskm/oyun-platformu`.
2. Branch: `main`.
3. Build pack: **Dockerfile**.
4. **Build context (Base directory): `/`** — repo kökü olmalı.
5. **Dockerfile location: `game-host/Dockerfile`**.
6. Port: **3000**. Healthcheck path: **`/healthz`**.
7. Domain: örn. `oyun.179.253.91.172.sslip.io` veya kendi alt domainin.
8. Deploy. Doğrulama:
   - `GET /healthz` → `ok`
   - `GET /games/gem-match/` → oyun HTML'i (`no-store`)
   - `GET /games/gem-match/assets/index-*.js` → JS (`immutable`)
   - `GET /api/query` → **404** olmalı

> Build context'i `game-host/` yaparsan Docker `../games` ve `../package.json`'u
> göremez. Bu yüzden context kök, Dockerfile `game-host/Dockerfile` olmalı.

## Telegram Mini App (tamamlandı)

- Bot: `@gemmatch_bot`
- Başlık: **Gem Match**
- Web App URL: `https://oyun.179.253.91.172.sslip.io/games/gem-match/`
- Kısa ad: `gemmatch`
- Açılış linki: https://t.me/gemmatch_bot/gemmatch

Menü butonu veya mesaj içinde "Oyunu Aç" düğmesi istersen Bot API tarafında
`short_name: gemmatch` ile WebApp butonu eklenir (bot token gerekir).

`?lang=tr` / `?lang=en` ile dili test edebilirsin. Dil, Telegram kullanıcı dilinden
veya menüdeki TR/EN düğmesinden de otomatik seçilir.

## Yeni oyun eklenince

`games/<id>/` build'i `dist/games/<id>/` altına yazdığı için bu sunucuya tek satır
ekleme gerekmez. `package.json` içindeki `build:games` komutuna yeni oyunun
`vite build` satırını eklemek yeterli (bkz. `OYUN-PLATFORMU.md`).


##############################################################################
# DOSYA: game-host/server.cjs
##############################################################################
'use strict';

/**
 * OYUN HOST — sadece oyun dosyalarını servis eden minimal sunucu.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Taskonomix'ten BAĞIMSIZDIR: veritabanı yok, /api yok, initData işlenmez.
 *
 * İki mod:
 *   GAME_ID yok  → tüm oyunları /games/<id>/ altında servis eder (ortak host)
 *   GAME_ID var  → sadece o oyunu kök dizinde servis eder (Coolify'da ayrı uygulama)
 *
 * Yerel:  node game-host/server.cjs
 *         GAME_ID=word-hunt node game-host/server.cjs
 */

const http = require('http');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const zlib = require('zlib');

const PORT = Number(process.env.PORT) || 3000;
const BIND = process.env.HOST || '0.0.0.0';
const GAME_ID = String(process.env.GAME_ID || '').trim().toLowerCase();

const candidates = [
  process.env.GAMES_ROOT,
  path.join(__dirname, 'dist', 'games'),
  path.join(__dirname, '..', 'dist', 'games'),
]
  .filter(Boolean)
  .map((p) => path.resolve(p));

const ROOT =
  candidates.find((p) => fs.existsSync(path.join(p, GAME_ID || 'gem-match', 'index.html'))) ||
  candidates.find((p) => fs.existsSync(path.join(p, 'gem-match', 'index.html'))) ||
  candidates.find((p) => fs.existsSync(path.join(p, 'word-hunt', 'index.html')));

if (!ROOT) {
  console.error('[oyun-host] oyun build bulunamadı. Önce `npm run build:games` çalıştır.');
  process.exit(1);
}

const MIME = {
  '.html': 'text/html; charset=utf-8',
  '.js': 'application/javascript; charset=utf-8',
  '.mjs': 'application/javascript; charset=utf-8',
  '.css': 'text/css; charset=utf-8',
  '.json': 'application/json; charset=utf-8',
  '.svg': 'image/svg+xml',
  '.png': 'image/png',
  '.jpg': 'image/jpeg',
  '.webp': 'image/webp',
  '.ico': 'image/x-icon',
  '.woff2': 'font/woff2',
};

const COMPRESSIBLE = /\.(html?|css|js|mjs|json|svg|xml)$/i;
const gzipCache = new Map();

function gzip(buffer, key) {
  const hit = gzipCache.get(key);
  if (hit) return hit;
  const out = zlib.gzipSync(buffer, { level: 9 });
  if (gzipCache.size < 40) gzipCache.set(key, out);
  return out;
}

function send(res, status, body, headers = {}) {
  const buf = Buffer.isBuffer(body) ? body : Buffer.from(String(body));
  res.writeHead(status, {
    'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff',
    'Referrer-Policy': 'no-referrer',
    'Content-Length': buf.length,
    ...headers,
  });
  res.end(buf);
}

function notFound(res) {
  send(res, 404, 'Not found', { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8', 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' });
}

function serveFile(req, res, file) {
  fs.stat(file, (err, stat) => {
    if (err || !stat.isFile()) {
      notFound(res);
      return;
    }
    fs.readFile(file, (readErr, data) => {
      if (readErr) {
        notFound(res);
        return;
      }
      const isIndex = path.basename(file) === 'index.html';
      const type = MIME[path.extname(file).toLowerCase()] || 'application/octet-stream';
      const headers = {
        'Content-Type': type,
        'Cache-Control': isIndex ? 'no-store' : 'public, max-age=31536000, immutable',
      };
      const acceptGzip = /\bgzip\b/i.test(String(req.headers['accept-encoding'] || ''));
      if (acceptGzip && COMPRESSIBLE.test(file)) {
        headers['Content-Encoding'] = 'gzip';
        headers.Vary = 'Accept-Encoding';
        send(res, 200, gzip(data, file), headers);
      } else {
        send(res, 200, data, headers);
      }
    });
  });
}

const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
  let pathname;
  try {
    pathname = decodeURIComponent(new URL(req.url, 'http://localhost').pathname);
  } catch {
    notFound(res);
    return;
  }

  if (pathname === '/healthz') {
    send(res, 200, 'ok', { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8', 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' });
    return;
  }

  if (GAME_ID && (pathname === '/' || pathname === '/index.html')) {
    serveFile(req, res, path.resolve(ROOT, GAME_ID, 'index.html'));
    return;
  }

  if (!GAME_ID && (pathname === '/' || pathname === '/games' || pathname === '/games/')) {
    res.writeHead(302, {
      Location: '/games/gem-match/',
      'Cache-Control': 'no-store',
      'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff',
    });
    res.end();
    return;
  }

  const match = /^\/games\/([a-z0-9-]+)\/(.*)$/i.exec(pathname);
  if (!match) {
    notFound(res);
    return;
  }

  const gameId = match[1];
  if (GAME_ID && gameId !== GAME_ID) {
    notFound(res);
    return;
  }

  const base = path.resolve(ROOT, gameId);
  let relative = match[2];
  if (!relative || relative.endsWith('/')) relative += 'index.html';

  const file = path.resolve(base, relative.replace(/^\/+/, ''));
  if (file !== path.join(base, 'index.html') && !file.startsWith(base + path.sep)) {
    notFound(res);
    return;
  }
  serveFile(req, res, file);
});

server.listen(PORT, BIND, () => {
  const pathLabel = GAME_ID ? '/' : `/games/${GAME_ID || 'gem-match'}/`;
  console.log(`[oyun-host] http://${BIND}:${PORT}${pathLabel}  (root: ${ROOT}, game: ${GAME_ID || 'all'})`);
});

process.on('SIGTERM', () => server.close(() => process.exit(0)));
process.on('SIGINT', () => server.close(() => process.exit(0)));

##############################################################################
# DOSYA: games/gem-match/src/core/board.ts
##############################################################################
/**
 * GEM MATCH — saf oyun mantığı.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Bu dosyada Phaser YOK. Tahta, eşleşme, yer çekimi ve hamle kontrolü
 * tamamen saf fonksiyonlar. Neden?
 *   - Test edilebilir (tarayıcı olmadan node ile doğrulanabilir)
 *   - Deterministik: aynı seed + aynı hamleler = aynı sonuç
 *   - İleride sunucuda replay doğrulaması yapılabilir
 */

import type { Rng } from '../../../shared/game-kit/src/rng.js';

export const COLS = 8;
export const ROWS = 8;
export const CELL_COUNT = COLS * ROWS;

export type Special = 'row' | 'col' | 'bomb' | 'rainbow' | null;

export interface Cell {
  color: number;
  special: Special;
}

export type Grid = Array<Cell | null>;

export const idx = (r: number, c: number): number => r * COLS + c;
export const rowOf = (i: number): number => Math.floor(i / COLS);
export const colOf = (i: number): number => i % COLS;

export function cloneGrid(grid: Grid): Grid {
  return grid.map((c) => (c ? { ...c } : null));
}

/** Aynı satır/sütunda komşu mu? */
export function isAdjacent(a: number, b: number): boolean {
  const dr = Math.abs(rowOf(a) - rowOf(b));
  const dc = Math.abs(colOf(a) - colOf(b));
  return dr + dc === 1;
}

export function swapCells(grid: Grid, a: number, b: number): void {
  const tmp = grid[a];
  grid[a] = grid[b];
  grid[b] = tmp;
}

export interface Run {
  dir: 'h' | 'v';
  cells: number[];
}

export interface MatchGroup {
  cells: number[];
  /** Bu gruptan doğacak özel taş tipi. */
  special: Special;
  /** Özel taşın doğacağı hücre. */
  anchor: number;
}

/** Yatay ve dikey 3+ zincirleri bulur. */
export function findRuns(grid: Grid): Run[] {
  const runs: Run[] = [];

  for (let r = 0; r < ROWS; r++) {
    let start = 0;
    for (let c = 1; c <= COLS; c++) {
      const prev = grid[idx(r, c - 1)];
      const cur = c < COLS ? grid[idx(r, c)] : null;
      const same = !!prev && !!cur && prev.color === cur.color;
      if (!same) {
        if (c - start >= 3) {
          const cells: number[] = [];
          for (let k = start; k < c; k++) cells.push(idx(r, k));
          runs.push({ dir: 'h', cells });
        }
        start = c;
      }
    }
  }

  for (let c = 0; c < COLS; c++) {
    let start = 0;
    for (let r = 1; r <= ROWS; r++) {
      const prev = grid[idx(r - 1, c)];
      const cur = r < ROWS ? grid[idx(r, c)] : null;
      const same = !!prev && !!cur && prev.color === cur.color;
      if (!same) {
        if (r - start >= 3) {
          const cells: number[] = [];
          for (let k = start; k < r; k++) cells.push(idx(k, c));
          runs.push({ dir: 'v', cells });
        }
        start = r;
      }
    }
  }

  return runs;
}

/**
 * Zincirleri birleştirip (L / T şekilleri) her grup için özel taş kararı verir.
 * `preferred` verilirse özel taş o hücrede doğar (oyuncunun oynadığı taş).
 */
export function findMatches(grid: Grid, preferred: number[] = []): MatchGroup[] {
  const runs = findRuns(grid);
  if (!runs.length) return [];

  type Bucket = { cells: Set<number>; runs: Run[] };
  const buckets: Bucket[] = [];

  for (const run of runs) {
    let target = buckets.find((b) => run.cells.some((c) => b.cells.has(c)));
    if (!target) {
      target = { cells: new Set<number>(), runs: [] };
      buckets.push(target);
    }
    for (const c of run.cells) target.cells.add(c);
    target.runs.push(run);

    // Bu zincir iki grubu birleştirdiyse (L/T) hepsini tek gruba indir.
    for (const other of buckets) {
      if (other === target || other.cells.size === 0) continue;
      let overlaps = false;
      for (const c of other.cells) {
        if (target.cells.has(c)) { overlaps = true; break; }
      }
      if (overlaps) {
        for (const c of other.cells) target.cells.add(c);
        target.runs.push(...other.runs);
        other.cells.clear();
        other.runs.length = 0;
      }
    }
  }

  const groups: MatchGroup[] = [];
  for (const b of buckets) {
    if (b.cells.size < 3) continue;
    const maxH = Math.max(0, ...b.runs.filter((r) => r.dir === 'h').map((r) => r.cells.length));
    const maxV = Math.max(0, ...b.runs.filter((r) => r.dir === 'v').map((r) => r.cells.length));
    const hasH = maxH >= 3;
    const hasV = maxV >= 3;

    let special: Special = null;
    if (maxH >= 5 || maxV >= 5) special = 'rainbow';
    else if (hasH && hasV) special = 'bomb';
    else if (maxH === 4) special = 'row';
    else if (maxV === 4) special = 'col';

    const cells = [...b.cells];
    const pref = cells.find((c) => preferred.includes(c));
    let anchor = pref;
    if (anchor === undefined) {
      const longest = [...b.runs].sort((x, y) => y.cells.length - x.cells.length)[0];
      anchor = longest ? longest.cells[Math.floor(longest.cells.length / 2)] : cells[0];
    }

    groups.push({ cells, special, anchor });
  }

  return groups;
}

/**
 * Özel taşları tetikler: patlama alanını genişletir (zincirleme dahil).
 * `swapColor`: gökkuşağı taşının sildiği renk.
 */
export function expandSpecials(
  grid: Grid,
  initial: Iterable<number>,
  swapColor?: number
): Set<number> {
  const out = new Set<number>(initial);
  const queue: number[] = [...out];
  const fired = new Set<number>();

  while (queue.length) {
    const i = queue.shift()!;
    const cell = grid[i];
    if (!cell || !cell.special || fired.has(i)) continue;
    fired.add(i);

    const add = (j: number) => {
      if (j < 0 || j >= CELL_COUNT) return;
      if (!grid[j]) return;
      if (!out.has(j)) {
        out.add(j);
        queue.push(j);
      } else if (grid[j]?.special && !fired.has(j)) {
        queue.push(j);
      }
    };

    const r = rowOf(i);
    const c = colOf(i);
    switch (cell.special) {
      case 'row':
        for (let k = 0; k < COLS; k++) add(idx(r, k));
        break;
      case 'col':
        for (let k = 0; k < ROWS; k++) add(idx(k, c));
        break;
      case 'bomb':
        for (let dr = -1; dr <= 1; dr++) {
          for (let dc = -1; dc <= 1; dc++) {
            const rr = r + dr;
            const cc = c + dc;
            if (rr >= 0 && rr < ROWS && cc >= 0 && cc < COLS) add(idx(rr, cc));
          }
        }
        break;
      case 'rainbow': {
        const targetColor = swapColor ?? cell.color;
        for (let k = 0; k < CELL_COUNT; k++) {
          if (grid[k] && grid[k]!.color === targetColor) add(k);
        }
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  return out;
}

/** Tahtada en az bir geçerli hamle var mı? */
export function findAnyMove(grid: Grid): [number, number] | null {
  // Gökkuşağı taşı varsa her zaman hamle vardır.
  for (let i = 0; i < CELL_COUNT; i++) {
    if (grid[i]?.special === 'rainbow') return [i, i];
  }
  for (let r = 0; r < ROWS; r++) {
    for (let c = 0; c < COLS; c++) {
      const a = idx(r, c);
      if (c + 1 < COLS) {
        const b = idx(r, c + 1);
        swapCells(grid, a, b);
        const ok = findMatches(grid).length > 0;
        swapCells(grid, a, b);
        if (ok) return [a, b];
      }
      if (r + 1 < ROWS) {
        const b = idx(r + 1, c);
        swapCells(grid, a, b);
        const ok = findMatches(grid).length > 0;
        swapCells(grid, a, b);
        if (ok) return [a, b];
      }
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/** Takas geçerli mi? (en az bir eşleşme üretiyor mu) */
export function swapCreatesMatch(grid: Grid, a: number, b: number): boolean {
  const ca = grid[a];
  const cb = grid[b];
  if (!ca || !cb) return false;
  // Gökkuşağı her zaman patlar.
  if (ca.special === 'rainbow' || cb.special === 'rainbow') return true;
  swapCells(grid, a, b);
  const ok = findMatches(grid).length > 0;
  swapCells(grid, a, b);
  return ok;
}

/** Başlangıç tahtası: hazır eşleşme yok, en az bir hamle var. */
export function createGrid(rng: Rng, colors: number): Grid {
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 60; attempt++) {
    const grid: Grid = new Array(CELL_COUNT).fill(null);
    for (let r = 0; r < ROWS; r++) {
      for (let c = 0; c < COLS; c++) {
        const banned = new Set<number>();
        const left1 = c >= 1 ? grid[idx(r, c - 1)] : null;
        const left2 = c >= 2 ? grid[idx(r, c - 2)] : null;
        if (left1 && left2 && left1.color === left2.color) banned.add(left1.color);
        const up1 = r >= 1 ? grid[idx(r - 1, c)] : null;
        const up2 = r >= 2 ? grid[idx(r - 2, c)] : null;
        if (up1 && up2 && up1.color === up2.color) banned.add(up1.color);
        const pool: number[] = [];
        for (let k = 0; k < colors; k++) if (!banned.has(k)) pool.push(k);
        grid[idx(r, c)] = { color: rng.pick(pool), special: null };
      }
    }
    if (findAnyMove(grid)) return grid;
  }
  // Aşırı düşük olasılık; son çare rastgele doldur.
  return Array.from({ length: CELL_COUNT }, () => ({
    color: rng.int(0, colors),
    special: null as Special,
  }));
}

export interface GravityResult {
  /** Mevcut taşların nereden nereye düştüğü. */
  moves: Array<{ to: number; from: number }>;
  /** Yukarıdan yeni doğan taşlar. */
  spawned: number[];
}

/**
 * Boşlukları kapatır ve üstten yeni taş üretir.
 * Girdi olarak null hücreler bekler (patlayan taşlar sahnede null yapılır).
 */
export function collapseAndRefill(
  grid: Grid,
  rng: Rng,
  colors: number
): GravityResult {
  const moves: Array<{ to: number; from: number }> = [];
  const spawned: number[] = [];

  for (let c = 0; c < COLS; c++) {
    let write = ROWS - 1;
    for (let r = ROWS - 1; r >= 0; r--) {
      const i = idx(r, c);
      const cell = grid[i];
      if (!cell) continue;
      const to = idx(write, c);
      if (to !== i) {
        grid[to] = cell;
        grid[i] = null;
        moves.push({ to, from: i });
      }
      write -= 1;
    }
    for (let r = write; r >= 0; r--) {
      const i = idx(r, c);
      grid[i] = { color: rng.int(0, colors), special: null };
      spawned.push(i);
    }
  }

  return { moves, spawned };
}

/** Patlayan taşlar için hızlı skor: her taş 10, combo çarpanı. */
export function clearScore(count: number, combo: number): number {
  const multiplier = 1 + (combo - 1) * 0.5;
  return Math.round(count * 10 * multiplier);
}


##############################################################################
# DOSYA: games/gem-match/src/core/levels.ts
##############################################################################
/**
 * Seviye eğrisi ve ödül kuralları.
 * NOT: Buradaki sayılar başlangıç değerleri. Gerçek kullanıcı verisiyle
 * dengelenecek. Hepsi tek yerde olduğu için ayar yapmak kolay.
 */

export interface LevelDef {
  level: number;
  moves: number;
  target: number;
  colors: number;
}

export const MAX_LEVEL = 40;

export function levelDef(level: number): LevelDef {
  const lv = Math.max(1, Math.min(MAX_LEVEL, Math.floor(level)));
  const moves = lv <= 3 ? 26 : lv <= 8 ? 24 : lv <= 15 ? 22 : 20;
  const target = Math.round(1500 + (lv - 1) * 350);
  const colors = lv <= 2 ? 5 : 6;
  return { level: lv, moves, target, colors };
}

export function starsFor(score: number, target: number): number {
  if (score >= target * 2) return 3;
  if (score >= target * 1.5) return 2;
  if (score >= target) return 1;
  return 0;
}

export function xpFor(level: number, score: number, stars: number): number {
  const base = 6 + level * 2 + stars * 7 + Math.floor(score / 1200) * 3;
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(150, base));
}

import Phaser from 'phaser';
import { BootScene } from './scenes/BootScene.js';
import { MenuScene } from './scenes/MenuScene.js';
import { GameScene } from './scenes/GameScene.js';
import { H, W } from './theme.js';
import { initTelegram, tgApp } from './telegram.js';
import './strings.js'; // metinleri kaydet (yan etki)
import { initLang } from './strings.js';

/**
 * Gem Match giriş noktası.
 * Tek başına çalışır: Taskonomix olmadan da açılır, oynanır, XP biriktirir.
 * Telegram Mini App olarak da tarayıcıda da aynı kod çalışır.
 */
const tgInfo = initTelegram();
initLang(tgInfo.language);

const game = new Phaser.Game({
  type: Phaser.AUTO,
  parent: 'game',
  backgroundColor: '#05070f',
  scale: {
    mode: Phaser.Scale.FIT,
    autoCenter: Phaser.Scale.CENTER_BOTH,
    width: W,
    height: H,
  },
  render: {
    antialias: true,
    powerPreference: 'high-performance',
  },
  fps: { target: 60 },
  scene: [BootScene, MenuScene, GameScene],
  audio: { noAudio: true }, // Ses game-kit içindeki Web Audio ile üretilir
});

const refresh = () => game.scale.refresh();
window.addEventListener('resize', refresh);
window.addEventListener('orientationchange', () => setTimeout(refresh, 250));

// Telegram viewport değişimleri (klavye, tam ekran, döndürme)
const tg = tgApp();
if (tg?.onEvent) {
  try {
    tg.onEvent('viewportChanged', refresh);
  } catch {
    /* yut */
  }
}

// Geliştirme kolaylığı: konsoldan sahne durumunu görmek için.
if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
  (window as any).__GEM = game;
  (window as any).__TG_INFO = tgInfo;
}


##############################################################################
# DOSYA: games/gem-match/src/scenes/BootScene.ts
##############################################################################
import Phaser from 'phaser';
import { GEMS } from '../theme.js';

/**
 * BOOT — tüm görseller kodla üretilir.
 * Avantaj: asset dosyası yok (telif/indirme/boyut sorunu yok), tek renk paletinden
 * tutarlı bir görünüm çıkar. İleride profesyonel sprite atlasına geçilirse
 * sadece bu dosya değişir.
 */
export class BootScene extends Phaser.Scene {
  constructor() {
    super('Boot');
  }

  create(): void {
    this.buildGems();
    this.buildSpecialIcons();
    this.buildFx();
    this.scene.start('Menu');
  }

  private buildGems(): void {
    const g = this.make.graphics({ x: 0, y: 0 }, false);
    const C = 32;
    const R = 29;

    GEMS.forEach((gem, i) => {
      g.clear();
      const poly = this.shapePoints(gem.shape, C, R);

      if (gem.shape === 3) {
        g.fillStyle(gem.dark, 1);
        g.fillRoundedRect(3, 3, 58, 58, 15);
        g.fillStyle(gem.main, 1);
        g.fillRoundedRect(5, 5, 54, 54, 13);
        g.fillStyle(gem.light, 0.28);
        g.fillRoundedRect(9, 9, 24, 20, 8);
        g.lineStyle(2, gem.light, 0.35);
        g.strokeRoundedRect(5, 5, 54, 54, 13);
      } else if (gem.shape === 5) {
        g.fillStyle(gem.dark, 1);
        g.fillCircle(C, C, R + 1);
        g.fillStyle(gem.main, 1);
        g.fillCircle(C, C, R - 1);
        g.fillStyle(gem.light, 0.32);
        g.fillCircle(C - 7, C - 8, 9);
        g.lineStyle(2, gem.light, 0.4);
        g.strokeCircle(C, C, R - 1);
      } else {
        // Alt gölge (derinlik hissi)
        g.fillStyle(gem.dark, 1);
        g.fillPoints(poly.map((p) => new Phaser.Geom.Point(p.x + 1, p.y + 2)), true);
        // Gövde
        g.fillStyle(gem.main, 1);
        g.fillPoints(poly, true);
        // Kenar
        g.lineStyle(2.5, gem.light, 0.45);
        g.strokePoints(poly, true);
        // Parlama
        const hi = poly.map(
          (p) => new Phaser.Geom.Point(C + (p.x - C) * 0.5 - 5, C + (p.y - C) * 0.5 - 7)
        );
        g.fillStyle(gem.light, 0.4);
        g.fillPoints(hi, true);
      }

      g.generateTexture(`gem${i}`, 64, 64);
    });

    g.destroy();
  }

  /** 0 elmas, 1 altıgen, 2 yıldız, 3 kare, 4 üçgen, 5 daire */
  private shapePoints(shape: number, cx: number, cy: number): Phaser.Geom.Point[] {
    const R = 29;
    const pt = (x: number, y: number) => new Phaser.Geom.Point(x, y);

    switch (shape) {
      case 0:
        return [pt(cx, cy - R), pt(cx + R, cy), pt(cx, cy + R), pt(cx - R, cy)];
      case 1: {
        const out: Phaser.Geom.Point[] = [];
        for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) {
          const a = (Math.PI / 3) * k - Math.PI / 2;
          out.push(pt(cx + Math.cos(a) * R, cy + Math.sin(a) * R));
        }
        return out;
      }
      case 2: {
        const out: Phaser.Geom.Point[] = [];
        for (let k = 0; k < 10; k++) {
          const a = (Math.PI / 5) * k - Math.PI / 2;
          const rr = k % 2 === 0 ? R + 1 : R * 0.45;
          out.push(pt(cx + Math.cos(a) * rr, cy + Math.sin(a) * rr));
        }
        return out;
      }
      case 4:
        return [pt(cx, cy - R), pt(cx + R, cy + R * 0.75), pt(cx - R, cy + R * 0.75)];
      default:
        return [pt(cx, cy - R), pt(cx + R, cy), pt(cx, cy + R), pt(cx - R, cy)];
    }
  }

  private buildSpecialIcons(): void {
    const g = this.make.graphics({ x: 0, y: 0 }, false);

    // Yatay patlama
    g.clear();
    g.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.95);
    g.fillRoundedRect(8, 27, 48, 10, 5);
    g.fillTriangle(2, 32, 14, 22, 14, 42);
    g.fillTriangle(62, 32, 50, 22, 50, 42);
    g.generateTexture('sp_row', 64, 64);

    // Dikey patlama
    g.clear();
    g.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.95);
    g.fillRoundedRect(27, 8, 10, 48, 5);
    g.fillTriangle(32, 2, 22, 14, 42, 14);
    g.fillTriangle(32, 62, 22, 50, 42, 50);
    g.generateTexture('sp_col', 64, 64);

    // Bomba
    g.clear();
    g.lineStyle(6, 0xffffff, 0.95);
    g.strokeCircle(32, 32, 17);
    g.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.95);
    g.fillCircle(32, 32, 7);
    g.generateTexture('sp_bomb', 64, 64);

    // Gökkuşağı
    g.clear();
    const cols = [0xff4d6d, 0xffb020, 0x2ee6a8, 0x4d9dff, 0xb46bff, 0xeaf6ff];
    cols.forEach((col, k) => {
      const a = (Math.PI * 2 * k) / cols.length;
      g.fillStyle(col, 1);
      g.fillCircle(32 + Math.cos(a) * 16, 32 + Math.sin(a) * 16, 7);
    });
    g.lineStyle(3, 0xffffff, 0.85);
    g.strokeCircle(32, 32, 26);
    g.generateTexture('sp_rainbow', 64, 64);

    g.destroy();
  }

  private buildFx(): void {
    const g = this.make.graphics({ x: 0, y: 0 }, false);

    g.fillStyle(0xffffff, 1);
    g.fillCircle(8, 8, 7);
    g.generateTexture('spark', 16, 16);

    g.clear();
    g.lineStyle(6, 0xffffff, 1);
    g.strokeCircle(64, 64, 58);
    g.generateTexture('ring', 128, 128);

    g.clear();
    g.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.9);
    g.fillCircle(16, 16, 15);
    g.generateTexture('dot', 32, 32);

    g.destroy();
  }
}


##############################################################################
# DOSYA: games/gem-match/src/scenes/GameScene.ts
##############################################################################
import Phaser from 'phaser';
import { H, LAYOUT, PALETTE, W, GEMS } from '../theme.js';
import { levelDef, MAX_LEVEL, starsFor, xpFor, type LevelDef } from '../core/levels.js';
import {
  CELL_COUNT,
  COLS,
  ROWS,
  clearScore,
  collapseAndRefill,
  colOf,
  createGrid,
  expandSpecials,
  findAnyMove,
  findMatches,
  idx,
  isAdjacent,
  rowOf,
  swapCells,
  swapCreatesMatch,
  type Cell,
  type Grid,
  type Special,
} from '../core/board.js';
import {
  createBridge,
  createRng,
  levelInfo,
  loadProfile,
  play,
  unlockAudio,
  type GameBridge,
  type SessionInfo,
} from '../../../shared/game-kit/src/index.js';
import type { Rng } from '../../../shared/game-kit/src/rng.js';
import { haptic, setBackButton } from '../telegram.js';
import { fmtInt, t, upper } from '../strings.js';

const FONT = '"Trebuchet MS", "Segoe UI", system-ui, sans-serif';

interface GemView {
  container: Phaser.GameObjects.Container;
  sprite: Phaser.GameObjects.Image;
  icon: Phaser.GameObjects.Image | null;
  color: number;
}

export class GameScene extends Phaser.Scene {
  private bridge!: GameBridge;
  private session!: SessionInfo;
  private rng!: Rng;
  private def!: LevelDef;

  private level = 1;
  private grid: Grid = [];
  private gems: Array<GemView | null> = [];
  private emitters: Phaser.GameObjects.Particles.ParticleEmitter[] = [];

  private score = 0;
  private movesLeft = 0;
  private combo = 0;
  private maxCombo = 0;
  private startedAt = 0;

  private selected: number | null = null;
  private selectionRing!: Phaser.GameObjects.Image;
  private dragStart: { x: number; y: number } | null = null;

  private ready = false;
  private busy = false;
  private ended = false;

  private scoreText!: Phaser.GameObjects.Text;
  private movesText!: Phaser.GameObjects.Text;
  private bar!: Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics;
  private comboText!: Phaser.GameObjects.Text;

  constructor() {
    super('Game');
  }

  init(data: { level?: number }): void {
    this.level = Math.max(1, Number(data?.level) || 1);
    this.def = levelDef(this.level);
    this.score = 0;
    this.movesLeft = this.def.moves;
    this.combo = 0;
    this.maxCombo = 0;
    this.selected = null;
    this.busy = false;
    this.ended = false;
    this.ready = false;
    this.grid = [];
    this.gems = [];
  }

  create(): void {
    this.bridge = createBridge('gem-match');
    this.buildBackground();
    this.buildHud();
    this.buildBoardFrame();

    this.selectionRing = this.add
      .image(0, 0, 'ring')
      .setDisplaySize(LAYOUT.cell - 4, LAYOUT.cell - 4)
      .setTint(0x3ee0ff)
      .setAlpha(0)
      .setDepth(9);

    this.input.on('pointerdown', this.onDown, this);
    this.input.on('pointermove', this.onMove, this);
    this.input.on('pointerup', this.onUp, this);

    // Telegram geri tuşu: oyunu yarıda bırakıp menüye döner (kayıt altına alınır).
    setBackButton(() => this.leaveLevel());
    this.events.once('shutdown', () => setBackButton(null));

    const loading = this.add
      .text(W / 2, 640, t('common.loading'), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '22px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setDepth(120);

    void this.bridge
      .start()
      .then((s) => {
        this.session = s;
        this.rng = createRng(s.seed);
        loading.destroy();
        this.begin();
      })
      .catch(() => {
        loading.setText(t('game.sessionError')).setAlign('center');
      });
  }

  // ================= KURULUM =================

  private buildBackground(): void {
    const g = this.add.graphics();
    g.fillGradientStyle(0x0a1226, 0x0a1226, 0x05070f, 0x05070f, 1);
    g.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    this.add
      .image(W / 2, 380, 'dot')
      .setDisplaySize(1000, 1000)
      .setAlpha(0.05)
      .setTint(0x3ee0ff)
      .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD);
  }

  private buildHud(): void {
    const panel = this.add.graphics();
    panel.fillStyle(0x0d1730, 0.95);
    panel.fillRoundedRect(28, 40, W - 56, 232, 26);
    panel.lineStyle(2, 0x1d2c50, 1);
    panel.strokeRoundedRect(28, 40, W - 56, 232, 26);

    this.add
      .text(56, 66, upper(t('game.levelLabel', { n: this.def.level })), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '18px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setLetterSpacing(2)
      .setDepth(100);

    this.movesText = this.add
      .text(W - 56, 62, '', {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '30px',
        color: PALETTE.text,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(1, 0)
      .setDepth(100);

    this.scoreText = this.add
      .text(W / 2, 138, '0', {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '64px',
        color: PALETTE.gold,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setDepth(100);

    this.bar = this.add.graphics().setDepth(100);

    this.comboText = this.add
      .text(W / 2, 232, '', {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '22px',
        color: PALETTE.accent,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setDepth(100)
      .setAlpha(0);

    this.updateHud();
  }

  private buildBoardFrame(): void {
    const w = COLS * LAYOUT.cell;
    const h = ROWS * LAYOUT.cell;
    const x = LAYOUT.boardX - w / 2;
    const g = this.add.graphics().setDepth(1);
    g.fillStyle(0x0b1428, 1);
    g.fillRoundedRect(x - 12, LAYOUT.boardTop - 12, w + 24, h + 24, 22);
    g.lineStyle(2, 0x1d2c50, 1);
    g.strokeRoundedRect(x - 12, LAYOUT.boardTop - 12, w + 24, h + 24, 22);

    // Hücre dokusu
    g.fillStyle(0x101d38, 0.55);
    for (let r = 0; r < ROWS; r++) {
      for (let c = 0; c < COLS; c++) {
        if ((r + c) % 2 === 0) continue;
        g.fillRoundedRect(
          x + c * LAYOUT.cell + 3,
          LAYOUT.boardTop + r * LAYOUT.cell + 3,
          LAYOUT.cell - 6,
          LAYOUT.cell - 6,
          10
        );
      }
    }
  }

  private begin(): void {
    this.grid = createGrid(this.rng, this.def.colors);
    this.buildGemViews();
    this.startedAt = Date.now();
    this.ready = true;
    this.bridge.track('level_start', { level: this.level });
  }

  private buildGemViews(): void {
    for (let i = 0; i < CELL_COUNT; i++) {
      const cell = this.grid[i];
      if (!cell) continue;
      const view = this.makeGem(i, cell);
      view.container.setAlpha(0);
      this.tweens.add({
        targets: view.container,
        alpha: 1,
        duration: 240,
        delay: rowOf(i) * 28 + colOf(i) * 12,
      });
    }
  }

  private makeGem(i: number, cell: Cell, startY?: number): GemView {
    const p = this.posOf(i);
    const sprite = this.add.image(0, 0, `gem${cell.color}`).setDisplaySize(62, 62);
    const container = this.add.container(p.x, startY ?? p.y, [sprite]);
    container.setSize(LAYOUT.cell, LAYOUT.cell);
    container.setDepth(10);

    const view: GemView = { container, sprite, icon: null, color: cell.color };
    if (cell.special) this.attachIconTo(view, cell.special);
    if (startY === undefined) {
      container.setScale(0.2);
      this.tweens.add({ targets: container, scale: 1, duration: 220, ease: 'Back.easeOut' });
    }
    this.gems[i] = view;
    return view;
  }

  private attachIconTo(view: GemView, special: Special): void {
    if (!special) return;
    if (view.icon) view.icon.destroy();
    const key =
      special === 'row' ? 'sp_row' : special === 'col' ? 'sp_col' : special === 'bomb' ? 'sp_bomb' : 'sp_rainbow';
    view.icon = this.add.image(0, 0, key).setDisplaySize(46, 46).setAlpha(0.92);
    view.container.add(view.icon);
    this.tweens.add({
      targets: view.icon,
      scale: { from: 0.85, to: 1 },
      duration: 900,
      yoyo: true,
      repeat: -1,
      ease: 'Sine.easeInOut',
    });
  }

  private attachIcon(i: number, special: Special): void {
    const view = this.gems[i];
    if (view) this.attachIconTo(view, special);
  }

  private posOf(i: number): { x: number; y: number } {
    const r = rowOf(i);
    const c = colOf(i);
    const left = LAYOUT.boardX - (COLS * LAYOUT.cell) / 2;
    return {
      x: left + LAYOUT.cell / 2 + c * LAYOUT.cell,
      y: LAYOUT.boardTop + LAYOUT.cell / 2 + r * LAYOUT.cell,
    };
  }

  // ================= GİRDİ =================

  private pointerCell(pointer: Phaser.Input.Pointer): number {
    const left = LAYOUT.boardX - (COLS * LAYOUT.cell) / 2;
    const top = LAYOUT.boardTop;
    const c = Math.floor((pointer.x - left) / LAYOUT.cell);
    const r = Math.floor((pointer.y - top) / LAYOUT.cell);
    if (r < 0 || r >= ROWS || c < 0 || c >= COLS) return -1;
    return idx(r, c);
  }

  private onDown(pointer: Phaser.Input.Pointer): void {
    unlockAudio();
    if (!this.ready || this.busy || this.ended) return;
    const cell = this.pointerCell(pointer);
    if (cell < 0) {
      this.clearSelection();
      return;
    }
    this.dragStart = { x: pointer.x, y: pointer.y };
    if (this.selected === null) {
      this.select(cell);
      return;
    }
    if (this.selected === cell) {
      this.clearSelection();
      return;
    }
    if (isAdjacent(this.selected, cell)) {
      const from = this.selected;
      this.dragStart = null;
      void this.trySwap(from, cell);
      return;
    }
    this.select(cell);
  }

  private onMove(pointer: Phaser.Input.Pointer): void {
    if (!this.ready || this.busy || this.ended) return;
    if (!pointer.isDown || this.selected === null || !this.dragStart) return;

    const dx = pointer.x - this.dragStart.x;
    const dy = pointer.y - this.dragStart.y;
    if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) < 18) return;

    const from = this.selected;
    let to = -1;
    if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) {
      const c = colOf(from) + (dx > 0 ? 1 : -1);
      if (c >= 0 && c < COLS) to = idx(rowOf(from), c);
    } else {
      const r = rowOf(from) + (dy > 0 ? 1 : -1);
      if (r >= 0 && r < ROWS) to = idx(r, colOf(from));
    }
    this.dragStart = null;
    if (to >= 0) void this.trySwap(from, to);
  }

  private onUp(): void {
    this.dragStart = null;
  }

  private select(i: number): void {
    this.selected = i;
    const p = this.posOf(i);
    this.selectionRing.setPosition(p.x, p.y).setAlpha(0.9).setScale(0.9);
    this.tweens.add({
      targets: this.selectionRing,
      scale: 1.05,
      alpha: 0.55,
      duration: 620,
      yoyo: true,
      repeat: -1,
      ease: 'Sine.easeInOut',
    });
    const view = this.gems[i];
    if (view) {
      this.tweens.add({ targets: view.container, scale: 1.12, duration: 110 });
    }
    play('select');
  }

  private clearSelection(): void {
    if (this.selected !== null) {
      const view = this.gems[this.selected];
      if (view) this.tweens.add({ targets: view.container, scale: 1, duration: 110 });
    }
    this.selected = null;
    this.tweens.killTweensOf(this.selectionRing);
    this.selectionRing.setAlpha(0);
  }

  // ================= HAMLE =================

  private async trySwap(a: number, b: number): Promise<void> {
    if (this.busy || this.ended || !this.ready) return;
    const ca = this.grid[a];
    const cb = this.grid[b];
    if (!ca || !cb) return;

    this.busy = true;
    this.clearSelection();

    const va = this.gems[a];
    const vb = this.gems[b];
    if (!va || !vb) {
      this.busy = false;
      return;
    }

    const pa = this.posOf(a);
    const pb = this.posOf(b);
    this.tweens.add({ targets: va.container, x: pb.x, y: pb.y, duration: 130, ease: 'Quad.easeOut' });
    this.tweens.add({ targets: vb.container, x: pa.x, y: pa.y, duration: 130, ease: 'Quad.easeOut' });
    play('swap');
    await this.wait(140);

    if (!swapCreatesMatch(this.grid, a, b)) {
      play('invalid');
      this.tweens.add({ targets: va.container, x: pa.x, y: pa.y, duration: 130 });
      this.tweens.add({ targets: vb.container, x: pb.x, y: pb.y, duration: 130 });
      await this.wait(150);
      this.busy = false;
      return;
    }

    const swapColor = ca.special === 'rainbow' ? cb.color : ca.color;

    swapCells(this.grid, a, b);
    this.gems[a] = vb;
    this.gems[b] = va;

    this.movesLeft = Math.max(0, this.movesLeft - 1);
    this.updateHud();

    await this.resolve([a, b], swapColor);
    await this.ensurePlayable();

    this.busy = false;
    this.checkEnd();
  }

  private async resolve(preferred: number[], swapColor?: number): Promise<void> {
    let guard = 0;
    let pref = preferred;
    let color = swapColor;

    while (guard++ < 40) {
      const groups = findMatches(this.grid, pref);
      if (!groups.length) break;

      this.combo += 1;
      this.maxCombo = Math.max(this.maxCombo, this.combo);

      const toClear = new Set<number>();
      for (const g of groups) for (const c of g.cells) toClear.add(c);

      // Gökkuşağı takası: taş eşleşmese de patlamalı.
      for (const p of pref) {
        if (this.grid[p]?.special === 'rainbow') toClear.add(p);
      }

      const spawns: Array<{ at: number; special: Special }> = [];
      for (const g of groups) {
        if (g.special) spawns.push({ at: g.anchor, special: g.special });
      }
      for (const s of spawns) toClear.delete(s.at);

      const cleared = expandSpecials(this.grid, toClear, color);
      for (const s of spawns) cleared.delete(s.at);
      if (!cleared.size && !spawns.length) break;

      const gained = clearScore(cleared.size, this.combo) + spawns.length * 40;
      this.score += gained;
      this.updateHud();
      this.showCombo(this.combo);

      await this.clearCells(cleared, this.combo);

      for (const s of spawns) {
        const cell = this.grid[s.at];
        if (cell && !cell.special) {
          cell.special = s.special;
          this.attachIcon(s.at, s.special);
          play('special');
          this.burst(this.posOf(s.at), 0xffd166, 10);
        }
      }

      await this.applyGravity();
      pref = [];
      color = undefined;
    }
    this.combo = 0;
  }

  private async clearCells(cells: Set<number>, combo: number): Promise<void> {
    if (!cells.size) return;
    play('match', combo);
    haptic(combo > 1 || cells.size >= 5 ? 'medium' : 'light');
    if (combo > 1 || cells.size >= 5) {
      this.cameras.main.shake(110, 0.0035);
    }

    for (const i of cells) {
      const view = this.gems[i];
      const cell = this.grid[i];
      if (!view || !cell) continue;
      const p = this.posOf(i);
      this.burst(p, GEMS[cell.color].main, combo > 1 ? 7 : 4);

      this.tweens.add({
        targets: view.container,
        scale: 0,
        angle: Phaser.Math.Between(-70, 70),
        alpha: 0.2,
        duration: 190,
        ease: 'Quad.easeIn',
        onComplete: () => view.container.destroy(),
      });
      this.grid[i] = null;
      this.gems[i] = null;
    }

    await this.wait(200);
  }

  private async applyGravity(): Promise<void> {
    const { moves, spawned } = collapseAndRefill(this.grid, this.rng, this.def.colors);

    for (const m of moves) {
      this.gems[m.to] = this.gems[m.from];
      this.gems[m.from] = null;
    }

    const duration = 250;
    for (const m of moves) {
      const view = this.gems[m.to];
      if (!view) continue;
      const p = this.posOf(m.to);
      this.tweens.add({
        targets: view.container,
        x: p.x,
        y: p.y,
        duration,
        ease: 'Bounce.easeOut',
      });
    }

    for (const i of spawned) {
      const cell = this.grid[i];
      if (!cell) continue;
      const p = this.posOf(i);
      const view = this.makeGem(i, cell, p.y - LAYOUT.cell * 2.2);
      this.tweens.add({
        targets: view.container,
        y: p.y,
        duration: duration + 60,
        ease: 'Bounce.easeOut',
      });
    }

    if (moves.length || spawned.length) play('fall');
    await this.wait(duration + 90);
  }

  private async ensurePlayable(): Promise<void> {
    if (findAnyMove(this.grid)) return;
    this.centerText(t('game.shuffling'));
    for (let i = 0; i < CELL_COUNT; i++) {
      this.gems[i]?.container.destroy();
      this.gems[i] = null;
    }
    this.grid = createGrid(this.rng, this.def.colors);
    this.buildGemViews();
    await this.wait(420);
  }

  // ================= GÖRSEL GERİ BİLDİRİM =================

  private burst(p: { x: number; y: number }, tint: number, count: number): void {
    const emitter = this.emitters.find((e) => (e as any)._tintValue === tint);
    if (emitter) {
      emitter.emitParticleAt(p.x, p.y, count);
    } else {
      const e = this.add.particles(0, 0, 'spark', {
        speed: { min: 60, max: 240 },
        angle: { min: 0, max: 360 },
        scale: { start: 0.85, end: 0 },
        lifespan: { min: 260, max: 560 },
        quantity: 1,
        tint,
        blendMode: 'ADD',
        emitting: false,
      });
      (e as any)._tintValue = tint;
      e.setDepth(60);
      this.emitters.push(e);
      e.emitParticleAt(p.x, p.y, count);
    }

    const ring = this.add
      .image(p.x, p.y, 'ring')
      .setDisplaySize(40, 40)
      .setTint(tint)
      .setAlpha(0.7)
      .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD)
      .setDepth(55);
    this.tweens.add({
      targets: ring,
      displayWidth: 96,
      displayHeight: 96,
      alpha: 0,
      duration: 320,
      onComplete: () => ring.destroy(),
    });
  }

  private floatText(x: number, y: number, text: string, color: string): void {
    const t = this.add
      .text(x, y, text, { fontFamily: FONT, fontSize: '24px', color, fontStyle: 'bold' })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setDepth(70);
    this.tweens.add({
      targets: t,
      y: y - 56,
      alpha: 0,
      duration: 620,
      ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
      onComplete: () => t.destroy(),
    });
  }

  private showCombo(combo: number): void {
    if (combo <= 1) return;
    this.comboText.setText(t('common.combo', { n: combo })).setAlpha(1).setScale(1.25);
    this.tweens.add({ targets: this.comboText, scale: 1, duration: 260, ease: 'Back.easeOut' });
    this.tweens.add({ targets: this.comboText, alpha: 0, delay: 520, duration: 320 });
  }

  private centerText(text: string): void {
    const t = this.add
      .text(W / 2, 620, text, { fontFamily: FONT, fontSize: '30px', color: PALETTE.accent, fontStyle: 'bold' })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setDepth(120)
      .setAlpha(0);
    this.tweens.add({ targets: t, alpha: 1, y: 600, duration: 220, yoyo: true, hold: 420, onComplete: () => t.destroy() });
  }

  private updateHud(): void {
    this.scoreText?.setText(fmtInt(this.score));
    this.movesText?.setText(t('common.movesLeft', { n: this.movesLeft }));
    if (this.scoreText) {
      this.tweens.add({ targets: this.scoreText, scale: { from: 1.12, to: 1 }, duration: 180 });
    }
    this.drawBar();
  }

  private drawBar(): void {
    if (!this.bar) return;
    const w = 480;
    const x = W / 2 - w / 2;
    const y = 196;
    const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, this.score / this.def.target));
    this.bar.clear();
    this.bar.fillStyle(0x16213f, 1);
    this.bar.fillRoundedRect(x, y, w, 14, 7);
    if (pct > 0) {
      this.bar.fillStyle(pct >= 1 ? 0x37e6a6 : 0x3ee0ff, 1);
      this.bar.fillRoundedRect(x, y, Math.max(14, w * pct), 14, 7);
    }
    this.bar.lineStyle(1, 0x2a3d68, 1);
    this.bar.strokeRoundedRect(x, y, w, 14, 7);
  }

  private wait(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      this.time.delayedCall(ms, () => resolve());
    });
  }

  /** Oyundan menüye dönüş — yarıda bırakma verisi de kaydedilir. */
  private leaveLevel(): void {
    if (this.ready && !this.ended) {
      this.bridge.track('quit', {
        level: this.level,
        score: this.score,
        movesLeft: this.movesLeft,
        progress: Math.min(1, this.score / this.def.target),
      });
    }
    this.ended = true;
    setBackButton(null);
    this.scene.start('Menu');
  }

  // ================= BİTİŞ =================

  private checkEnd(): void {
    if (this.ended) return;
    if (this.movesLeft > 0) return;
    void this.endGame();
  }

  private async endGame(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.ended) return;
    this.ended = true;
    this.busy = true;
    this.clearSelection();

    const won = this.score >= this.def.target;
    const stars = starsFor(this.score, this.def.target);
    const xp = xpFor(this.level, this.score, stars);
    play(won ? 'win' : 'lose');
    haptic(won ? 'success' : 'error');
    this.cameras.main.flash(won ? 220 : 160, won ? 255 : 120, won ? 220 : 40, won ? 150 : 40);

    let xpGained = xp;
    try {
      const outcome = await this.bridge.finish({
        score: this.score,
        durationMs: Date.now() - this.startedAt,
        level: won ? this.level : Math.max(0, this.level - 1),
        xp,
        stats: {
          stars,
          movesUsed: this.def.moves - this.movesLeft,
          maxCombo: this.maxCombo,
          target: this.def.target,
        },
        clientVersion: '0.1.0',
      });
      xpGained = outcome.xpGained;
    } catch {
      /* bağlantı yoksa yerel sonuç gösterilir */
    }

    this.bridge.track('level_end', {
      level: this.level,
      score: this.score,
      won,
      stars,
      maxCombo: this.maxCombo,
    });

    this.showResult(won, stars, xpGained);
  }

  private showResult(won: boolean, stars: number, xpGained: number): void {
    const profile = loadProfile();
    const info = levelInfo(profile.xp);
    const best = profile.games['gem-match']?.bestScore ?? this.score;

    const layer = this.add.container(0, 0).setDepth(200);
    const dim = this.add
      .rectangle(0, 0, W, H, 0x03060d, 0.82)
      .setOrigin(0)
      .setInteractive();
    layer.add(dim);

    const panelY = 380;
    const panel = this.add.graphics();
    panel.fillStyle(0x0e1930, 1);
    panel.fillRoundedRect(70, panelY, W - 140, 520, 30);
    panel.lineStyle(2, won ? 0x2f7a5f : 0x4a2130, 1);
    panel.strokeRoundedRect(70, panelY, W - 140, 520, 30);
    layer.add(panel);

    const title = this.add
      .text(W / 2, panelY + 58, upper(won ? t('game.clear') : t('game.fail')), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '38px',
        color: won ? PALETTE.success : PALETTE.danger,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5);
    layer.add(title);

    // Yıldızlar
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const x = W / 2 + (i - 1) * 96;
      const earned = i < stars;
      const star = this.add
        .image(x, panelY + 152, 'gem2')
        .setDisplaySize(earned ? 78 : 62, earned ? 78 : 62)
        .setTint(earned ? 0xffd166 : 0x24365f)
        .setAlpha(earned ? 1 : 0.5);
      layer.add(star);
      if (earned) {
        star.setScale(0);
        this.tweens.add({
          targets: star,
          scale: 1,
          duration: 380,
          delay: 220 + i * 160,
          ease: 'Back.easeOut',
        });
      }
    }

    const lines: Array<[string, string, string]> = [
      [upper(t('common.score')), fmtInt(this.score), PALETTE.text],
      [upper(t('common.target')), fmtInt(this.def.target), PALETTE.textDim],
      [upper(t('common.best')), fmtInt(best), PALETTE.gold],
      [upper(t('common.xpEarned')), `+${xpGained}`, PALETTE.accent],
    ];
    lines.forEach(([label, value, color], i) => {
      const y = panelY + 232 + i * 46;
      layer.add(
        this.add
          .text(110, y, label, { fontFamily: FONT, fontSize: '19px', color: PALETTE.textDim, fontStyle: 'bold' })
          .setOrigin(0, 0.5)
      );
      layer.add(
        this.add
          .text(W - 110, y, value, { fontFamily: FONT, fontSize: '24px', color, fontStyle: 'bold' })
          .setOrigin(1, 0.5)
      );
    });

    const buttons: Array<{ label: string; primary?: boolean; action: () => void }> = [
      { label: upper(t('common.menu')), action: () => this.scene.start('Menu') },
      { label: upper(t('common.retry')), action: () => this.scene.restart({ level: this.level }) },
    ];
    if (won && this.level < MAX_LEVEL) {
      buttons.push({
        label: upper(t('common.next')),
        primary: true,
        action: () => this.scene.restart({ level: this.level + 1 }),
      });
    } else {
      buttons[1].primary = true;
    }

    const totalW = W - 200;
    const gap = 14;
    const bw = (totalW - gap * (buttons.length - 1)) / buttons.length;
    buttons.forEach((b, i) => {
      const bx = 100 + i * (bw + gap) + bw / 2;
      const by = panelY + 452;
      const g = this.add.graphics();
      g.fillStyle(b.primary ? 0x1ec8ea : 0x18243f, 1);
      g.fillRoundedRect(bx - bw / 2, by - 30, bw, 60, 18);
      if (b.primary) {
        g.fillStyle(0x3ee0ff, 1);
        g.fillRoundedRect(bx - bw / 2, by - 32, bw, 56, 18);
      }
      layer.add(g);

      const label = this.add
        .text(bx, by - 4, b.label, {
          fontFamily: FONT,
          fontSize: '21px',
          color: b.primary ? '#04222b' : PALETTE.text,
          fontStyle: 'bold',
        })
        .setOrigin(0.5);
      layer.add(label);

      const zone = this.add
        .zone(bx, by, bw, 60)
        .setOrigin(0.5)
        .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
      zone.on('pointerdown', () => {
        unlockAudio();
        play('click');
        b.action();
      });
      layer.add(zone);
    });

    layer.add(
      this.add
        .text(W / 2, panelY + 505, t('common.levelOf', { level: info.level, title: info.title }), {
          fontFamily: FONT,
          fontSize: '16px',
          color: PALETTE.textDim,
        })
        .setOrigin(0.5)
    );

    layer.setAlpha(0);
    this.tweens.add({ targets: layer, alpha: 1, duration: 260 });
  }
}


##############################################################################
# DOSYA: games/gem-match/src/scenes/MenuScene.ts
##############################################################################
import Phaser from 'phaser';
import { GEMS, H, PALETTE, W } from '../theme.js';
import { levelDef, MAX_LEVEL } from '../core/levels.js';
import { levelInfo, loadProfile, isMuted, setMuted, play, unlockAudio } from '../../../shared/game-kit/src/index.js';
import { closeApp, haptic, setBackButton, tgApp } from '../telegram.js';
import { fmtInt, getLang, t, toggleLang, upper } from '../strings.js';

const FONT = '"Trebuchet MS", "Segoe UI", system-ui, sans-serif';

export class MenuScene extends Phaser.Scene {
  private level = 1;
  private requestedLevel = 0;
  private levelText!: Phaser.GameObjects.Text;
  private targetText!: Phaser.GameObjects.Text;
  private mutedLabel!: Phaser.GameObjects.Text;

  constructor() {
    super('Menu');
  }

  init(data: { level?: number }): void {
    this.requestedLevel = Math.max(0, Number(data?.level) || 0);
  }

  create(): void {
    this.level = this.requestedLevel || this.nextLevel();
    this.buildBackground();
    this.buildTitle();
    this.buildLevelCard();
    this.buildPlayButton();
    this.buildProfileBar();
    this.buildSoundToggle();
    this.buildLangToggle();

    // Telegram: geri tuşu uygulamayı kapatır (menüde başka gidilecek yer yok).
    setBackButton(() => {
      haptic('light');
      closeApp();
    });

    this.add
      .text(W / 2, H - 34, t('menu.footer'), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '16px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setAlpha(0.7);

    this.cameras.main.fadeIn(320, 7, 11, 24);
  }

  private nextLevel(): number {
    const p = loadProfile();
    const best = p.games['gem-match']?.bestLevel ?? 0;
    return Math.max(1, Math.min(MAX_LEVEL, best + 1));
  }

  private buildBackground(): void {
    const g = this.add.graphics();
    g.fillGradientStyle(0x0a1226, 0x0a1226, 0x05070f, 0x05070f, 1);
    g.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    // Yumuşak ışık
    this.add
      .image(W / 2, 300, 'dot')
      .setDisplaySize(900, 900)
      .setAlpha(0.07)
      .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD)
      .setTint(0x3ee0ff);

    // Yıldız alanı
    for (let i = 0; i < 70; i++) {
      const s = this.add
        .image(Phaser.Math.Between(0, W), Phaser.Math.Between(0, H), 'dot')
        .setDisplaySize(Phaser.Math.Between(2, 5), Phaser.Math.Between(2, 5))
        .setAlpha(Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0.08, 0.4));
      this.tweens.add({
        targets: s,
        alpha: Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0.05, 0.25),
        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(1400, 3600),
        yoyo: true,
        repeat: -1,
        delay: Phaser.Math.Between(0, 1500),
      });
    }

    // Dekoratif taşlar
    GEMS.forEach((gem, i) => {
      const x = 60 + ((i * 137) % 600);
      const y = 1080 + (i % 3) * 46;
      const img = this.add.image(x, y, `gem${i}`).setDisplaySize(46, 46).setAlpha(0.22);
      this.tweens.add({
        targets: img,
        y: y - 16,
        angle: Phaser.Math.Between(-12, 12),
        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(2200, 3400),
        yoyo: true,
        repeat: -1,
        ease: 'Sine.easeInOut',
        delay: i * 120,
      });
    });
  }

  private buildTitle(): void {
    const shadow = this.add
      .text(W / 2 + 3, 262, 'GEM MATCH', {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '76px',
        color: PALETTE.accent,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setAlpha(0.55);

    const title = this.add
      .text(W / 2, 258, 'GEM MATCH', {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '76px',
        color: PALETTE.gold,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5);

    this.tweens.add({
      targets: [title, shadow],
      y: '-=8',
      duration: 2000,
      yoyo: true,
      repeat: -1,
      ease: 'Sine.easeInOut',
    });

    this.add
      .text(W / 2, 322, t('menu.tagline'), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '19px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setLetterSpacing(2);
  }

  private buildLevelCard(): void {
    const g = this.add.graphics();
    g.fillStyle(0x0d1730, 0.92);
    g.fillRoundedRect(W / 2 - 270, 400, 540, 190, 26);
    g.lineStyle(2, 0x24365f, 1);
    g.strokeRoundedRect(W / 2 - 270, 400, 540, 190, 26);

    this.add
      .text(W / 2, 434, upper(t('menu.levelLabel')), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '17px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setLetterSpacing(3);

    this.levelText = this.add
      .text(W / 2, 500, String(this.level), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '64px',
        color: PALETTE.text,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5);

    this.targetText = this.add
      .text(W / 2, 556, '', {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '19px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5);

    const chevron = (x: number, label: string, dir: number) => {
      const t = this.add
        .text(x, 500, label, {
          fontFamily: FONT,
          fontSize: '54px',
          color: PALETTE.accent,
          fontStyle: 'bold',
        })
        .setOrigin(0.5)
        .setAlpha(0.85)
        .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
      t.on('pointerdown', () => {
        unlockAudio();
        play('click');
        const next = Phaser.Math.Clamp(this.level + dir, 1, this.nextLevel());
        if (next !== this.level) {
          this.level = next;
          this.refreshLevel();
        }
      });
      return t;
    };
    chevron(W / 2 - 210, '‹', -1);
    chevron(W / 2 + 210, '›', 1);

    this.refreshLevel();
  }

  private refreshLevel(): void {
    const def = levelDef(this.level);
    this.levelText.setText(String(def.level));
    this.targetText.setText(
      t('menu.target', { score: fmtInt(def.target), moves: def.moves })
    );
    this.tweens.add({
      targets: this.levelText,
      scale: { from: 1.18, to: 1 },
      duration: 220,
      ease: 'Back.easeOut',
    });
  }

  private buildPlayButton(): void {
    const y = 700;
    const glow = this.add.graphics();
    glow.fillStyle(0x3ee0ff, 0.18);
    glow.fillRoundedRect(W / 2 - 230, y - 66, 460, 132, 34);

    const g = this.add.graphics();
    g.fillStyle(0x1ec8ea, 1);
    g.fillRoundedRect(W / 2 - 210, y - 55, 420, 110, 28);
    g.fillStyle(0x3ee0ff, 1);
    g.fillRoundedRect(W / 2 - 210, y - 58, 420, 100, 28);

    const label = this.add
      .text(W / 2, y - 8, upper(t('common.play')), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '44px',
        color: '#04222b',
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setLetterSpacing(4);

    const zone = this.add
      .zone(W / 2, y - 8, 420, 110)
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });

    zone.on('pointerdown', () => {
      unlockAudio();
      play('click');
      this.cameras.main.fadeOut(220, 7, 11, 24);
      this.cameras.main.once('camerafadeoutcomplete', () => {
        this.scene.start('Game', { level: this.level });
      });
    });

    this.tweens.add({
      targets: [g, glow, label],
      y: '+=5',
      duration: 1600,
      yoyo: true,
      repeat: -1,
      ease: 'Sine.easeInOut',
    });
  }

  private buildProfileBar(): void {
    const p = loadProfile();
    const info = levelInfo(p.xp);
    const stats = p.games['gem-match'];
    const barW = 460;
    const x = W / 2 - barW / 2;
    const y = 900;

    const tgUser = tgApp()?.initDataUnsafe?.user;
    if (tgUser) {
      const name = [tgUser.first_name, tgUser.last_name].filter(Boolean).join(' ').trim() || tgUser.username;
      if (name) {
        this.add
          .text(W / 2, y - 40, t('common.welcome', { name }), {
            fontFamily: FONT,
            fontSize: '18px',
            color: PALETTE.accent,
            fontStyle: 'bold',
          })
          .setOrigin(0.5)
          .setAlpha(0.9);
      }
    }

    this.add
      .text(W / 2, y, t('common.levelOf', { level: info.level, title: info.title }), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '22px',
        color: PALETTE.text,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5);

    const track = this.add.graphics();
    track.fillStyle(0x16213f, 1);
    track.fillRoundedRect(x, y + 32, barW, 16, 8);
    if (info.progress > 0) {
      const fill = this.add.graphics();
      fill.fillStyle(0x3ee0ff, 1);
      fill.fillRoundedRect(x, y + 32, Math.max(16, barW * info.progress), 16, 8);
      fill.setAlpha(0.9);
    }

    this.add
      .text(W / 2, y + 64, `${info.xpIntoLevel} / ${info.xpForNext} XP`, {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '16px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5);

    if (stats && stats.bestScore > 0) {
      this.add
        .text(W / 2, y + 104, `${t('common.best')}: ${fmtInt(stats.bestScore)}`, {
          fontFamily: FONT,
          fontSize: '20px',
          color: PALETTE.gold,
          fontStyle: 'bold',
        })
        .setOrigin(0.5);
    }
  }

  private buildSoundToggle(): void {
    this.mutedLabel = this.add
      .text(W - 24, 30, this.soundLabel(), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '15px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(1, 0)
      .setAlpha(0.85)
      .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });

    this.mutedLabel.on('pointerdown', () => {
      unlockAudio();
      const next = !isMuted();
      setMuted(next);
      this.mutedLabel.setText(this.soundLabel());
      if (!next) play('click');
    });
  }

  private soundLabel(): string {
    return upper(isMuted() ? t('common.soundOff') : t('common.soundOn'));
  }

  /** Dil seçici: sol üstte TR / EN. Dokununca menü aynı bölümde yeniden çizilir. */
  private buildLangToggle(): void {
    const y = 30;
    this.add
      .text(74, y, '/', {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '16px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5, 0)
      .setAlpha(0.6);

    const chip = (label: string, x: number) => {
      const text = this.add
        .text(x, y, label, {
          fontFamily: FONT,
          fontSize: '17px',
          color: PALETTE.textDim,
          fontStyle: 'bold',
        })
        .setOrigin(0.5, 0)
        .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
      text.on('pointerdown', () => {
        unlockAudio();
        play('click');
        if (getLang() !== label.toLowerCase()) toggleLang();
        this.scene.restart({ level: this.level });
      });
      return text;
    };

    const tr = chip('TR', 46);
    const en = chip('EN', 102);
    const active = getLang();
    tr.setColor(active === 'tr' ? PALETTE.accent : PALETTE.textDim).setAlpha(active === 'tr' ? 1 : 0.6);
    en.setColor(active === 'en' ? PALETTE.accent : PALETTE.textDim).setAlpha(active === 'en' ? 1 : 0.6);
  }
}

##############################################################################
# DOSYA: games/gem-match/src/telegram.ts
##############################################################################
/**
 * TELEGRAM MINI APP KATMANI
 * ------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Oyun Telegram içinde açıldığında:
 *   - tam ekrana yayılır (expand)
 *   - dikey kaydırma kapanır (oyun tahtası kaymasın)
 *   - geri tuşu oyun içinde menüye döner
 *   - eşleşmelerde dokunsal geri bildirim (haptic) verilir
 *
 * Telegram dışında (tarayıcıda) açılırsa hepsi sessizce devre dışı kalır.
 */

type TgUser = {
  id?: number;
  first_name?: string;
  last_name?: string;
  username?: string;
  language_code?: string;
};
type WebApp = any;

let backHandler: (() => void) | null = null;

export function tgApp(): WebApp | null {
  return (globalThis as any).Telegram?.WebApp ?? null;
}

export function isTelegram(): boolean {
  const w = tgApp();
  return !!w && !!w.initData;
}

export interface TgInfo {
  inTelegram: boolean;
  userName: string | null;
  /** Telegram kullanıcı dili (tr, en, tr-TR...) — dil seçimi için ipucu. */
  language: string | null;
}

export function initTelegram(): TgInfo {
  const w = tgApp();
  if (!w) return { inTelegram: false, userName: null, language: null };

  try {
    w.ready();
    w.expand();
    if (typeof w.disableVerticalSwipes === 'function') w.disableVerticalSwipes();
    if (typeof w.setHeaderColor === 'function') w.setHeaderColor('#05070f');
    if (typeof w.setBackgroundColor === 'function') w.setBackgroundColor('#05070f');
  } catch {
    /* eski Bot API sürümleri */
  }

  const u: TgUser | undefined = w.initDataUnsafe?.user;
  const userName = u
    ? [u.first_name, u.last_name].filter(Boolean).join(' ').trim() || u.username || null
    : null;

  return {
    inTelegram: true,
    userName,
    language: u?.language_code ?? null,
  };
}

/** Geri tuşunu sahneye bağlar. null → gizle. */
export function setBackButton(handler: (() => void) | null): void {
  const w = tgApp();
  if (!w?.BackButton) return;
  try {
    if (backHandler) w.BackButton.offClick(backHandler);
    backHandler = handler;
    if (handler) {
      w.BackButton.onClick(handler);
      w.BackButton.show();
    } else {
      w.BackButton.hide();
    }
  } catch {
    /* yut */
  }
}

export type HapticKind = 'light' | 'medium' | 'heavy' | 'success' | 'error' | 'warning';

export function haptic(kind: HapticKind = 'light'): void {
  const h = tgApp()?.HapticFeedback;
  if (!h) return;
  try {
    if (kind === 'success' || kind === 'error' || kind === 'warning') h.notificationOccurred(kind);
    else h.impactOccurred(kind);
  } catch {
    /* yut */
  }
}

export function closeApp(): void {
  const w = tgApp();
  try {
    w?.close?.();
  } catch {
    /* yut */
  }
}
