  private starsLit = 0;
  private shownScore = -1;
  private lastWordAt = 0;

  // Fırsatlar: joker, ateş, altın an, hazine kelimesi, rozetler, günlük
  private jokers = new Set<number>();
  private treasure = '';
  private treasureText!: Phaser.GameObjects.Text;
  private modeText!: Phaser.GameObjects.Text;
  private feverG!: Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics;
  private modeFx!: Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics;
  private feverMeter = 0;
  private feverUntil = 0;
  private goldenUntil = 0;
  private goldenFired = new Set<number>();
  private shownMode = '';
  private meta!: Meta;
  private daily = false;
  private resultNote = '';
  private toastQueue: string[] = [];
  private toasting = false;

  constructor() {
    super('Game');
  }

  init(data: { level?: number; daily?: boolean }): void {
    this.daily = !!data?.daily;
    this.level = this.daily ? dailyLevel(dayKey(Date.now())) : Math.max(1, Number(data?.level) || 1);
    this.def = levelDef(this.level);
    this.score = 0;
    this.secondsLeft = this.def.seconds;
    this.combo = 0;
    this.maxCombo = 0;
    this.found = new Set();
    this.foundOrder = [];
    this.path = [];
    this.ready = false;
    this.ended = false;
    this.board = [];
    this.hexViews = [];
    this.hexGlows = [];
    this.hexShadows = [];
    this.freezeUntil = 0;
    this.feverTinted = false;
    this.shownTimeColor = '';
    this.letterTexts = [];
    this.hexOnFlags = [];
    this.hintsLeft = HINTS_PER_LEVEL;
    this.hintCount = 0;
    this.bonus = new Set();
    this.bonusMarks = [];
    this.starMarks = [];
    this.starsLit = 0;
    this.shownScore = -1;
    this.lastWordAt = 0;
    this.jokers = new Set();
    this.treasure = '';
    this.feverMeter = 0;
    this.feverUntil = 0;
    this.goldenUntil = 0;
    this.goldenFired = new Set();
    this.shownMode = '';
    this.resultNote = '';
    this.toastQueue = [];
    this.toasting = false;
  }

  create(): void {
    this.lang = getLang();
    this.meta = loadMeta();
    this.bridge = createBridge('word-hunt');
    this.buildBackground();
    this.buildHud();
    this.buildBoard();
    this.buildFx();

    this.input.on('pointerdown', this.onDown, this);
    this.input.on('pointermove', this.onMove, this);
    this.input.on('pointerup', this.onUp, this);

    setBackButton(() => this.leaveLevel());
    this.events.once('shutdown', () => {
      setBackButton(null);
      this.timerEvent?.remove();
    });

    const loading = this.add
      .text(W / 2, 640, t('common.loading'), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '22px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setDepth(120);

    void this.bridge
      .start()
      .then((s) => {
        this.session = s;
        this.rng = createRng(this.daily ? dailySeed(dayKey(Date.now())) : s.seed);
        loading.destroy();
        this.begin();
      })
      .catch(() => {
        loading.setText(t('game.sessionError')).setAlign('center');
      });
  }

  // ================= KURULUM =================

  private buildBackground(): void {
    const g = this.add.graphics();
    g.fillGradientStyle(0x0f1b3a, 0x0f1b3a, 0x04060d, 0x04060d, 1);
    g.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    this.add
      .image(W / 2, 640, 'dot')
      .setDisplaySize(1100, 1100)
      .setAlpha(0.14)
      .setTint(0xffb347)
      .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD);
    this.add
      .image(110, 1190, 'dot')
      .setDisplaySize(720, 720)
      .setAlpha(0.1)
      .setTint(0x2dd4bf)
      .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD);

    // Yavaşça süzülen derinlik petekleri (görsel rastgelelik de seed'li).
    const vr = createRng(0xbee5);
    for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) {
      const x = vr.next() * W;
      const y = vr.next() * H;
      const hex = this.add
        .image(x, y, 'hex')
        .setScale(0.35 + vr.next() * 0.8)
        .setAlpha(0.05 + vr.next() * 0.07)
        .setTint(0x4a7bd6)
        .setAngle(vr.int(0, 60));
      this.tweens.add({
        targets: hex,
        y: y - 160 - vr.next() * 200,
        angle: hex.angle + 40,
        alpha: 0.02,
        duration: 14000 + vr.int(0, 12000),
        yoyo: true,
        repeat: -1,
        ease: 'Sine.easeInOut',
      });
    }

    // Son 10 saniyede kenarlarda kırmızı nabız.
    this.dangerFx = this.add.rectangle(0, 0, W, H, 0xff1744, 1).setOrigin(0).setDepth(90).setAlpha(0);
  }

  private buildFx(): void {
    this.burst = this.add
      .particles(0, 0, 'spark', {
        lifespan: { min: 380, max: 840 },
        speed: { min: 90, max: 360 },
        angle: { min: 0, max: 360 },
        scale: { start: 0.9, end: 0 },
        alpha: { start: 1, end: 0 },
        gravityY: 380,
        tint: [0xffd166, 0xff9f43, 0xfff3c4, 0x7dffe0],
        blendMode: Phaser.BlendModes.ADD,
        emitting: false,
      })
      .setDepth(250);

    // Konfeti: yıldız dokulu, yukarı fırlayıp düşen renkli parçalar.
    this.confetti = this.add
      .particles(0, 0, 'star5', {
        lifespan: { min: 800, max: 1500 },
        speed: { min: 140, max: 560 },
        angle: { min: -150, max: -30 },
        gravityY: 720,
        scale: { start: 0.24, end: 0.06 },
        rotate: { min: 0, max: 360 },
        alpha: { start: 1, end: 0 },
        tint: [0xffd166, 0xff6a3d, 0x7dffe0, 0xff8fc7, 0xa78bfa, 0xffffff],
        emitting: false,
      })
      .setDepth(251);

    this.treasureText = this.add
      .text(W / 2, 1252, '', {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '17px',
        color: PALETTE.accent2,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setDepth(30)
      .setLetterSpacing(2);
    this.modeFx = this.add.graphics().setDepth(91);
  }

  private buildHud(): void {
    const panel = this.add.graphics();
    panel.fillStyle(0x000000, 0.3);
    panel.fillRoundedRect(30, 48, W - 56, 184, 28);
    panel.fillStyle(0x14223f, 0.94);
    panel.fillRoundedRect(28, 40, W - 56, 184, 28);
    panel.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.05);
    panel.fillRoundedRect(28, 40, W - 56, 72, { tl: 28, tr: 28, bl: 0, br: 0 });
    panel.lineStyle(2, 0x34507f, 1);
    panel.strokeRoundedRect(28, 40, W - 56, 184, 28);
    panel.lineStyle(1.5, 0xffd166, 0.18);
    panel.strokeRoundedRect(31, 43, W - 62, 178, 25);

    this.add
      .text(56, 66, upper(t('game.levelLabel', { n: this.def.level })), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '18px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setLetterSpacing(2)
      .setDepth(100);

    this.timeText = this.add
      .text(W - 56, 62, '', {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '28px',
        color: PALETTE.text,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(1, 0)
      .setDepth(100);

    this.scoreText = this.add
      .text(W / 2, 118, '0', {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '58px',
        color: PALETTE.accent,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
        stroke: '#5a3a00',
        strokeThickness: 4,
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setDepth(100);

    // Kombo rozeti + sönme çubuğu (update() içinde çizilir)
    this.comboText = this.add
      .text(56, 98, '', {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '22px',
        color: PALETTE.accent2,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setDepth(100);
    this.comboG = this.add.graphics().setDepth(100);
    this.modeText = this.add
      .text(W - 56, 100, '', {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '20px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(1, 0)
      .setDepth(100);
    this.feverG = this.add.graphics().setDepth(100);

    this.bar = this.add.graphics().setDepth(100);
    // Yıldız işaretleri: hedef (1★), %140 (2★), %200 (3★). Çubuk 0 → 2×hedef.
    this.starMarks = [0.5, 0.7, 1].map((f) =>
      this.add
        .image(W / 2 - 240 + 480 * f, 175, 'star5')
        .setDisplaySize(24, 24)
        .setTint(0x24365f)
        .setDepth(102)
    );

    this.foundText = this.add
      .text(W / 2, 198, '', {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '17px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setDepth(100);

    this.updateHud();
  }

  private buildBoard(): void {
    const size = boardSize();
    this.originX = LAYOUT.boardX - size.w / 2;
    this.originY = LAYOUT.boardTop;

    const frame = this.add.graphics();
    frame.fillStyle(0xffd166, 0.035);
    frame.fillRoundedRect(this.originX - 28, this.originY - 28, size.w + 56, size.h + 56, 34);
    frame.fillStyle(0x0a1226, 0.88);
    frame.fillRoundedRect(this.originX - 16, this.originY - 16, size.w + 32, size.h + 32, 24);
    frame.lineStyle(2, 0x6a552e, 0.9);
    frame.strokeRoundedRect(this.originX - 16, this.originY - 16, size.w + 32, size.h + 32, 24);

    // İp peteklerin ÜSTÜNDE, harflerin altında kalsın ki seçim yolu görünsün.
    this.pathG = this.add.graphics().setDepth(9);

    // Tahtanın üstünden periyodik geçen cam parlaması.
    const maskG = this.make.graphics({ x: 0, y: 0 }, false);
    maskG.fillStyle(0xffffff, 1);
    maskG.fillRoundedRect(this.originX - 16, this.originY - 16, size.w + 32, size.h + 32, 24);
    const sweep = this.add
      .image(this.originX - 120, this.originY + size.h / 2, 'dot')
      .setDisplaySize(90, size.h + 220)
      .setAngle(18)
      .setTint(0xfff3c4)
      .setAlpha(0.16)
      .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD)
      .setDepth(11);
    sweep.setMask(maskG.createGeometryMask());
    this.tweens.add({
      targets: sweep,
      x: this.originX + size.w + 140,
      duration: 1700,
      ease: 'Sine.easeInOut',
      repeat: -1,
      repeatDelay: 4200,
    });

    this.wordText = this.add
      .text(W / 2, 1160, '', {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '46px',
        color: PALETTE.accent,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setDepth(30);

    this.feedbackText = this.add
      .text(W / 2, 1210, t('game.minLength', { n: this.def.minLength }), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '18px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setDepth(30);

    this.hintG = this.add.graphics().setDepth(8);
    this.buildHintButton();
  }

  // ================= İPUCU =================

  private buildHintButton(): void {
    const btn = addButton(this, W - 100, 1166, 160, 64, '', { fontSize: 20, border: 0xffd166, labelColor: PALETTE.accent }, () =>
      this.useHint()
    );
    btn.container.setDepth(30);
    this.hintText = btn.label;
    this.refreshHintLabel();
  }

  private refreshHintLabel(): void {
    this.hintText?.setText(upper(t('game.hintLeft', { n: this.hintsLeft })));
    this.hintText?.setAlpha(this.hintsLeft > 0 ? 1 : 0.4);
  }

  private useHint(): void {
    if (!this.ready || this.ended) return;
    if (this.hintsLeft <= 0) {
      this.feedbackText.setText(t('game.hintNone')).setColor(PALETTE.danger);
      play('invalid');
      return;
    }
    const hint = findHint(this.board, this.planted, this.found);
    if (!hint) {
      this.feedbackText.setText(t('game.hintEmpty')).setColor(PALETTE.textDim);
      return;
    }
    this.hintsLeft -= 1;
    this.hintCount += 1;
    this.refreshHintLabel();
    play('hint');
    haptic('light');
    this.bridge.track('hint_used', { level: this.level, length: hint.word.length });

    // Sadece ilk iki hücreyi ve yönü göster; kelimeyi tamamen verme.
    const shown = hint.path.slice(0, 2);
    this.hintG.clear();
    this.hintG.lineStyle(6, 0xffd166, 0.95);
    this.hintG.beginPath();
    shown.forEach((i, k) => {
      const p = this.posOf(i);
      if (k === 0) this.hintG.moveTo(this.originX + p.x, this.originY + p.y);
      else this.hintG.lineTo(this.originX + p.x, this.originY + p.y);
    });
    this.hintG.strokePath();
    this.hintG.setAlpha(1);
    this.tweens.add({ targets: this.hintG, alpha: 0, duration: 400, delay: 1800 });
    this.feedbackText
      .setText(t('game.hintLength', { n: hint.word.length }))
      .setColor(PALETTE.accent);
  }

  private begin(): void {
    const gen = generateBoard(this.rng, this.lang, this.def.plantCount);
    this.board = gen.cells;
    this.planted = gen.planted;
    // Bonus petekler tahtadan SONRA seçilir: aynı seed → aynı tahta + aynı bonuslar.
    this.bonus = new Set(pickBonusCells(this.rng, bonusCount(this.level)));
    this.jokers = new Set(pickBonusCells(this.rng, jokerCount(this.level), COLS * ROWS, this.bonus));
    // Hazine kelimesi: ekilen en uzun kelime (eşitlikte ekim sırası → deterministik).
    this.treasure = [...this.planted].filter((w) => w.length >= 5).sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length)[0] ?? '';
    play('whoosh');

    for (let i = 0; i < COLS * ROWS; i++) {
      const p = this.posOf(i);
      const x = this.originX + p.x;
      const y = this.originY + p.y;

      const glow = this.add
        .image(x, y, 'dot')
        .setDisplaySize(HEX_SIZE * 2.9, HEX_SIZE * 2.9)
        .setTint(0xffd166)
        .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD)
        .setAlpha(0)
        .setDepth(4);

      // Zemin gölgesi: petek yükselince büyüyüp koyulaşır (3B derinlik).
      const shadow = this.add
        .image(x, y + 9, 'dot')
        .setDisplaySize(HEX_SIZE * 1.95, HEX_SIZE * 1.1)
        .setTint(0x000000)
        .setAlpha(0)
        .setDepth(5);

      const img = this.add
        .image(x, y, this.baseTex(i))
        .setDisplaySize(HEX_SIZE * 2 + 28, HEX_SIZE * 2 + 28)
        .setDepth(6)
        .setAlpha(0)
        .setScale(0.35);

      const letter = this.add
        .text(x, y - 3, this.jokers.has(i) ? '★' : display(this.board[i].letter, this.lang), {
          fontFamily: FONT,
          fontSize: '38px',
          color: PALETTE.hexText,
          fontStyle: 'bold',
          stroke: '#04202a',
          strokeThickness: 3,
        })
        .setOrigin(0.5)
        .setDepth(10)
        .setAlpha(0);

      const mark = this.add
        .image(x + HEX_SIZE * 0.5, y - HEX_SIZE * 0.58, 'star5')
        .setDisplaySize(22, 22)
        .setTint(0xf6dcff)
        .setDepth(12)
        .setAlpha(0);

      this.hexGlows.push(glow);
      this.hexShadows.push(shadow);
      this.hexViews.push(img);
      this.letterTexts.push(letter);
      this.bonusMarks.push(mark);
      this.hexOnFlags.push(false);

      const delay = (i % COLS) * 22 + Math.floor(i / COLS) * 26;
      this.tweens.add({ targets: img, alpha: 1, scale: 1, duration: 420, ease: 'Back.easeOut', delay });
      this.tweens.add({ targets: letter, alpha: 1, duration: 280, delay: delay + 120 });
      this.tweens.add({ targets: shadow, alpha: 0.3, duration: 400, delay: delay + 60 });
    }
    for (const i of this.bonus) this.setBonus(i, true, 700);
    this.refreshTreasure();

    this.startedAt = Date.now();
    this.lastWordAt = this.time.now;
    this.ready = true;
    this.bridge.track('level_start', { level: this.level, lang: this.lang });

    this.timerEvent = this.time.addEvent({
      delay: 1000,
      loop: true,
      callback: () => {
        if (this.ended) return;
        // Zaman dondurma: süre akmaz.
        if (this.time.now < this.freezeUntil) return;
        this.secondsLeft = Math.max(0, this.secondsLeft - 1);
        this.updateHud();
        this.maybeGolden();
        if (this.secondsLeft > 0 && this.secondsLeft <= 10) this.dangerPulse();
        if (this.secondsLeft === 0) void this.endGame();
      },
    });
  }

  private posOf(i: number): { x: number; y: number } {
    return hexPos(i);
  }

  // ================= GİRDİ =================

  private cellAt(x: number, y: number): number {
    let best = -1;
    let bestDist = (HEX_SIZE * 0.94) ** 2;
    for (let i = 0; i < this.board.length; i++) {
      const p = this.posOf(i);
      const dx = this.originX + p.x - x;
      const dy = this.originY + p.y - y;
      const d = dx * dx + dy * dy;
      if (d < bestDist) {
        bestDist = d;
        best = i;
      }
    }
    return best;
  }

  private onDown(pointer: Phaser.Input.Pointer): void {
    unlockAudio();
    if (!this.ready || this.ended) return;
    const cell = this.cellAt(pointer.x, pointer.y);
    if (cell < 0) return;
    this.path = [cell];
    this.refreshPath();
    play('select', this.path.length);
  }

  private onMove(pointer: Phaser.Input.Pointer): void {
    if (!this.ready || this.ended || !pointer.isDown || this.path.length === 0) return;
    const cell = this.cellAt(pointer.x, pointer.y);
    if (cell < 0) return;
    const last = this.path[this.path.length - 1];
    if (cell === last) return;

    if (this.path.length >= 2 && cell === this.path[this.path.length - 2]) {
      this.path.pop();
      this.refreshPath();
      return;
    }
    if (this.path.includes(cell)) return;
    if (!isAdjacent(last, cell)) return;

    this.path.push(cell);
    this.refreshPath();
    play('select', this.path.length);
  }

  private onUp(): void {
    if (!this.ready || this.ended || this.path.length === 0) return;
    const result = checkPathJoker(this.board, this.path, this.jokers, this.lang, this.def.minLength, this.found);
    if (result.ok) this.acceptWord(result);
    else this.rejectWord(result);
    this.clearPath();
  }

  private acceptWord(result: CheckResult): void {
    const cells = [...this.path];
    const bonusUsed = cells.filter((i) => this.bonus.has(i));
    const jokerCells = cells.filter((i) => this.jokers.has(i));
    const len = result.word.length;
    const now = this.time.now;

    this.found.add(result.word);
    this.foundOrder.push(result.word);
    this.combo += 1;
    this.maxCombo = Math.max(this.maxCombo, this.combo);
    this.lastWordAt = now;

    const isTreasure = this.treasure !== '' && result.word === this.treasure;
    const fever = now < this.feverUntil;
    const golden = now < this.goldenUntil;
    const mult = multiplierFor(fever, golden, isTreasure);
    const reward = lengthReward(len);
    const gained = scoreWord(len, this.combo, this.level, bonusUsed.length) * mult + reward.points;
    this.score += gained;

    const extra = timeBonusFor(len, bonusUsed.length) + (isTreasure ? 5 : 0) + (fever ? 1 : 0);
    if (extra > 0) {
      this.secondsLeft = Math.min(this.def.seconds + MAX_EXTRA_SECONDS, this.secondsLeft + extra);
    }
    const tier = tierFor(len);

    let cx = 0;
    let cy = 0;
    for (const i of cells) {
      const p = this.posOf(i);
      cx += this.originX + p.x;
      cy += this.originY + p.y;
    }
    cx /= cells.length;
    cy /= cells.length;

    this.wordText.setText(display(result.word, this.lang));
    this.feedbackText
      .setText(`+${fmtInt(gained)}  ${t(`game.tier${tier}`)}`)
      .setColor(PALETTE.success);

    play('word', len);
    if (this.combo >= 3) play('match', this.combo);
    haptic(tier >= 2 || this.combo >= 3 ? 'success' : 'light');

    this.flashPath(cells);
    this.popCells(cells, tier);
    this.burst.emitParticleAt(cx, cy, 14 + len * 4);
    this.popup(cx, cy - 24, `+${fmtInt(gained)}`, tier >= 2 || mult > 1 ? '#ffd166' : '#37e6a6', 34 + tier * 8);
    if (mult > 1) this.popup(cx, cy + 22, `x${mult}`, '#ff9f43', 30);
    if (extra > 0) {
      this.popup(W - 120, 108, upper(t('game.bonusTime', { n: extra })), '#7dffe0', 26);
    }

    // Bonus petekler kullanılınca yeni yerlere taşınır.
    if (bonusUsed.length) {
      play('bonus');
      for (const i of bonusUsed) {
        this.bonus.delete(i);
        this.setBonus(i, false);
      }
      const taken = new Set<number>([...this.bonus, ...this.jokers, ...cells]);
      for (const i of pickBonusCells(this.rng, bonusUsed.length, COLS * ROWS, taken)) {
        this.bonus.add(i);
        this.setBonus(i, true);
      }
    }

    // Joker sadece kelimeyi tamamlamak için gerçekten gerekliyse harcanır.
    if (result.joker && jokerCells.length) {
      play('bonus');
      this.popup(cx, cy + 62, t('game.joker'), '#ff8fc7', 30);
      for (const i of jokerCells) {
        this.jokers.delete(i);
        this.setJoker(i, false);
      }
      const taken = new Set<number>([...this.bonus, ...this.jokers, ...cells]);
      for (const i of pickBonusCells(this.rng, jokerCells.length, COLS * ROWS, taken)) {
        this.jokers.add(i);
        this.setJoker(i, true);
      }
    }

    // Hazine kelimesi: büyük kutlama.
    if (isTreasure) {
      play('treasure');
      this.banner(t('game.treasureFound'), '#ffd166');
      this.cameras.main.flash(240, 255, 215, 120);
      this.burst.emitParticleAt(W / 2, 640, 90);
      this.meta.stats.treasures += 1;
    }

    // Uzun kelime ödülü: 6+ harfte görünür kutlama, 7+ ipucu ve zaman dondurma.
    if (len >= 6) {
      this.popup(cx, cy - 74, upper(t('game.longReward')), '#ffd166', 24);
      this.confetti.emitParticleAt(cx, cy, 24 + len * 6);
      this.shockwave(cx, cy, 0xffd166);
      if (reward.hints > 0) {
        this.hintsLeft = Math.min(9, this.hintsLeft + reward.hints);
        this.refreshHintLabel();
        this.popup(cx, cy - 104, t('game.hintGain', { n: reward.hints }), '#7dffe0', 24);
      }
      if (reward.freezeMs > 0) {
        this.freezeUntil = Math.max(this.freezeUntil, now) + reward.freezeMs;
        play('freeze');
        this.banner(t('game.frozen'), '#7dd3fc');
      }
    }

    // Kombo kilometre taşı: belirli kombolarda ekstra süre ve puan.
    const milestone = comboMilestone(this.combo);
    if (milestone) {
      this.score += milestone.points;
      this.secondsLeft = Math.min(this.def.seconds + MAX_EXTRA_SECONDS, this.secondsLeft + milestone.seconds);
      this.popup(
        W / 2,
        330,
        `${t('game.comboMilestone', { n: this.combo })}  ${t('game.bonusTime', { n: milestone.seconds })}`,
        '#ff9f43',
        38
      );
      this.confetti.emitParticleAt(W / 2, 360, 30);
      play('star', 3);
    }

    // Ateş sayacı dolar; dolunca ATEŞ MODU başlar.
    if (!fever) {
      this.feverMeter = Math.min(100, this.feverMeter + feverGain(len, bonusUsed.length) + reward.fever);
      if (this.feverMeter >= 100) this.startFever();
    }

    if (tier >= 2) this.cameras.main.shake(130, 0.003 + tier * 0.001);
    if (tier >= 3) {
      this.cameras.main.flash(180, 255, 215, 120);
      this.burst.emitParticleAt(W / 2, 640, 60);
      play('whoosh');
    }

    // Rozet istatistikleri
    const st = this.meta.stats;
    st.totalWords += 1;
    st.longestWord = Math.max(st.longestWord, len);
    st.bestCombo = Math.max(st.bestCombo, this.maxCombo);
    if (result.joker) st.jokerWords += 1;

    // Günlük görevler
    const missionsDone = addMissionProgress(this.meta, dayKey(Date.now()), {
      words: 1,
      long: len >= 6 ? 1 : 0,
      combo: this.combo,
    });
    for (const id of missionsDone) this.toastQueue.push(`mis:${id}`);
    this.checkBadges();

    this.updateHud();

    this.bridge.track('word_found', {
      level: this.level,
      word: result.word,
      length: len,
      points: gained,
      combo: this.combo,
      bonus: bonusUsed.length,
      extraSeconds: extra,
      mult,
      joker: !!result.joker,
      treasure: isTreasure,
    });

    const list = this.foundOrder.slice(-4).map((w) => display(w, this.lang)).join('  ·  ');
    this.time.delayedCall(900, () => {
      if (this.ended) return;
      this.feedbackText.setText(list).setColor(PALETTE.textDim);
      this.wordText.setText('');
    });
  }

  private rejectWord(result: CheckResult): void {
    const msg =
      result.reason === 'short'
        ? t('common.tooShort')
        : result.reason === 'duplicate'
          ? t('common.alreadyFound')
          : t('common.notAWord');
    this.feedbackText.setText(msg).setColor(PALETTE.danger);
    play('invalid');
    haptic('error');
    this.cameras.main.shake(90, 0.0025);
    // Geçersiz kelime komboyu bozar; kısa/tekrar denemeler bozmaz.
    if (result.reason === 'invalid' && this.combo > 0) {
      this.combo = 0;
      this.updateHud();
    }
  }

  // ================= EFEKTLER =================

  private ring(x: number, y: number, color: number): void {
    const r = this.add
      .image(x, y, 'ring')
      .setTint(color)
      .setAlpha(0.9)
      .setScale(0.5)
      .setDepth(20)
      .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD);
    this.tweens.add({
      targets: r,
      scale: 1.7,
      alpha: 0,
      duration: 380,
      ease: 'Cubic.easeOut',
      onComplete: () => r.destroy(),
    });
  }

  private popup(x: number, y: number, text: string, color: string, size: number): void {
    const tx = this.add
      .text(x, y, text, {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: `${size}px`,
        color,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
        stroke: '#05070f',
        strokeThickness: 6,
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setDepth(80);
    this.tweens.add({
      targets: tx,
      y: y - 80,
      alpha: 0,
      scale: 1.2,
      duration: 900,
      ease: 'Cubic.easeOut',
      onComplete: () => tx.destroy(),
    });
  }

  /** Bulunan kelimenin yolu altın renkte parlayıp söner. */
  private flashPath(cells: number[]): void {
    const g = this.add.graphics().setDepth(9);
    g.lineStyle(12, 0xffd166, 0.95);
    g.beginPath();
    cells.forEach((i, k) => {
      const p = this.posOf(i);
      const x = this.originX + p.x;
      const y = this.originY + p.y;
      if (k === 0) g.moveTo(x, y);
      else g.lineTo(x, y);
    });
    g.strokePath();
    this.tweens.add({ targets: g, alpha: 0, duration: 420, onComplete: () => g.destroy() });
  }

  /** Bonus peteğin mor dokusunu ve nabız atan yıldızını açar/kapatır. */
  private setBonus(i: number, on: boolean, delay = 0): void {
    const mark = this.bonusMarks[i];
    const img = this.hexViews[i];
    if (!mark || !img) return;
    this.tweens.killTweensOf(mark);
    if (!this.hexOnFlags[i]) img.setTexture(this.baseTex(i));
    if (on) {
      mark.setAlpha(0).setScale(22 / 64);
      this.tweens.add({
        targets: mark,
        scale: 0.46,
        alpha: { from: 0.55, to: 1 },
        duration: 700,
        yoyo: true,
        repeat: -1,
        delay,
        ease: 'Sine.easeInOut',
      });
    } else {
      mark.setAlpha(0);
    }
  }

  private dangerPulse(): void {
    play('tick');
    this.tweens.add({ targets: this.timeText, scale: { from: 1.3, to: 1 }, duration: 280 });
    this.dangerFx.setAlpha(0.2);
    this.tweens.add({ targets: this.dangerFx, alpha: 0, duration: 700 });
  }

  private celebrate(): void {
    const spots: Array<[number, number]> = [
      [W * 0.25, 520],
      [W * 0.75, 520],
      [W / 2, 420],
    ];
    spots.forEach(([x, y], k) =>
      this.time.delayedCall(k * 180, () => {
        this.burst.emitParticleAt(x, y, 48);
        this.ring(x, y, 0xffd166);
      })
    );
  }

  /** Her kare: ateş sayacı, mod çerçevesi ve kombo çubuğu. */
  update(): void {
    if (!this.comboG) return;
    const now = this.time.now;
    const feverOn = now < this.feverUntil;
    const goldenOn = now < this.goldenUntil;
    const frozen = now < this.freezeUntil;

    // Ateş modunda petekler sıcak tonda parlar.
    if (feverOn !== this.feverTinted) {
      this.feverTinted = feverOn;
      for (const img of this.hexViews) {
        if (feverOn) img.setTint(0xffc9a0);
        else img.clearTint();
      }
    }
    const timeColor = frozen ? '#7dd3fc' : this.secondsLeft <= 10 ? PALETTE.danger : PALETTE.text;
    if (timeColor !== this.shownTimeColor) {
      this.shownTimeColor = timeColor;
      this.timeText?.setColor(timeColor);
    }

    if (this.feverUntil > 0 && !feverOn) {
      this.feverUntil = 0;
      this.feverMeter = 0;
    }
    if (feverOn) this.feverMeter = Math.max(0, (100 * (this.feverUntil - now)) / FEVER_MS);

    // Ateş çubuğu (sağ üst)
    this.feverG.clear();
    this.feverG.fillStyle(0x24365f, 1);
    this.feverG.fillRoundedRect(W - 206, 130, 150, 6, 3);
    if (this.feverMeter > 0) {
      this.feverG.fillStyle(feverOn ? 0xff5d3c : 0xff9f43, 1);
      this.feverG.fillRoundedRect(W - 206, 130, Math.max(6, 1.5 * this.feverMeter), 6, 3);
    }

    // Mod etiketi: ATEŞ / ATEŞ x2 / ALTIN x2 ...
    const mult = multiplierFor(feverOn, goldenOn, false);
    let label = upper(t('game.fever'));
    if (mult > 1) label = `${feverOn ? upper(t('game.fever')) : upper(t('game.golden').replace('!', ''))}  x${mult}`;
    if (frozen) label = upper(t('game.frozen'));
    if (label !== this.shownMode) {
      this.shownMode = label;
      this.modeText.setText(label).setColor(frozen ? '#7dd3fc' : mult > 1 ? PALETTE.accent2 : PALETTE.textDim);
    }

    // Ekran çerçevesi nabzı
    this.modeFx.clear();
    if ((feverOn || goldenOn || frozen) && !this.ended) {
      const a = 0.32 + 0.18 * Math.sin(now / 120);
      this.modeFx.lineStyle(14, frozen ? 0x7dd3fc : feverOn ? 0xff6a3d : 0xffd166, a);
      this.modeFx.strokeRect(7, 7, W - 14, H - 14);
    }

    // Kombo çubuğu
    this.comboG.clear();
    if (this.combo <= 0 || this.ended) return;
    const left = 1 - (now - this.lastWordAt) / COMBO_WINDOW_MS;
    if (left <= 0) {
      this.combo = 0;
      this.updateHud();
      return;
    }
    this.comboG.fillStyle(0x24365f, 1);
    this.comboG.fillRoundedRect(56, 130, 150, 6, 3);
    this.comboG.fillStyle(left < 0.3 ? 0xff5d6c : 0xff9f43, 1);
    this.comboG.fillRoundedRect(56, 130, Math.max(6, 150 * left), 6, 3);
  }

  // ================= FIRSATLAR =================

  private baseTex(i: number): string {
    return this.jokers.has(i) ? 'hexJoker' : this.bonus.has(i) ? 'hexBonus' : 'hex';
  }

  private setJoker(i: number, on: boolean): void {
    const img = this.hexViews[i];
    const letter = this.letterTexts[i];
    if (!img || !letter) return;
    if (!this.hexOnFlags[i]) img.setTexture(this.baseTex(i));
    letter.setText(on ? '★' : display(this.board[i].letter, this.lang));
  }

  private startFever(): void {
    this.feverUntil = this.time.now + FEVER_MS;
    this.feverMeter = 100;
    this.meta.stats.fevers += 1;
    play('fever');
    haptic('success');
    this.banner(t('game.feverOn'), '#ff9f43');
    this.cameras.main.flash(200, 255, 120, 50);
  }

  /** Süre eşiklerinde (≈%60 ve ≈%28) 8 sn'lik ALTIN AN: ekstra +1 çarpan. */
  private maybeGolden(): void {
    if (this.def.seconds < 60) return;
    for (const frac of [0.6, 0.28]) {
      const mark = Math.floor(this.def.seconds * frac);
      if (this.secondsLeft === mark && !this.goldenFired.has(mark)) {
        this.goldenFired.add(mark);
        this.goldenUntil = this.time.now + GOLDEN_MS;
        play('bonus');
        this.banner(t('game.golden'), '#ffd166');
      }
    }
  }

  private banner(text: string, color: string): void {
    const tx = this.add
      .text(W / 2, 640, text, {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '64px',
        color,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
        stroke: '#05070f',
        strokeThickness: 10,
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setDepth(120)
      .setScale(0.5)
      .setAlpha(0);
    this.tweens.add({ targets: tx, scale: 1, alpha: 1, duration: 280, ease: 'Back.easeOut' });
    this.tweens.add({
      targets: tx,
      alpha: 0,
      y: 600,
      duration: 500,
      delay: 1100,
      onComplete: () => tx.destroy(),
    });
  }

  /** Hazine kelimesinin harfleri süre ilerledikçe açılır. */
  private refreshTreasure(): void {
    if (!this.treasureText) return;
    if (!this.treasure) {
      this.treasureText.setText('');
      return;
    }
    const found = this.found.has(this.treasure);
    const reveal = found
      ? this.treasure.length
      : this.secondsLeft <= this.def.seconds * 0.25
        ? 2
        : this.secondsLeft <= this.def.seconds * 0.5
          ? 1
          : 0;
    const slots = display(this.treasure, this.lang)
      .split('')
      .map((ch, k) => (k < reveal ? ch : '_'))
      .join(' ');
    this.treasureText
      .setText(`${upper(t('game.treasure'))}  ${slots}`)
      .setColor(found ? PALETTE.success : PALETTE.accent2);
  }

  /** Kelimedeki her petek sırayla "patlar": zıplar, halka yayar, parçacık saçar. */
  private popCells(cells: number[], tier: number): void {
    cells.forEach((i, k) => {
      const p = this.posOf(i);
      const x = this.originX + p.x;
      const y = this.originY + p.y;
      this.time.delayedCall(k * 45, () => {
        if (!this.hexViews[i]) return;
        play('pop', k + 1);
        this.burst.emitParticleAt(x, y, 7 + tier * 4);
        this.ring(x, y, tier >= 2 ? 0xff9f43 : 0x7dffe0);
        this.tweens.add({
          targets: this.hexViews[i],
          scale: { from: 1.32, to: 1 },
          duration: 340,
          ease: 'Back.easeOut',
        });
      });
    });
  }

  /** Büyük, ince bir şok dalgası halkası (uzun kelimelerde). */
  private shockwave(x: number, y: number, color: number): void {
    for (let k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
      const r = this.add
        .image(x, y, 'ring')
        .setTint(color)
        .setAlpha(0.8)
        .setScale(0.4)
        .setDepth(19)
        .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD);
      this.tweens.add({
        targets: r,
        scale: 5.5,
        alpha: 0,
        duration: 700,
        delay: k * 140,
        ease: 'Cubic.easeOut',
        onComplete: () => r.destroy(),
      });
    }
  }

  /** Kazanınca 3 sandıktan birini seç: XP ödülü. Süre dolarsa ortadaki açılır. */
  private pickChest(stars: number): Promise<number> {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      const values = chestValues(stars);
      this.rng.shuffle(values);

      const layer = this.add.container(0, 0).setDepth(210);
      const dim = this.add.rectangle(0, 0, W, H, 0x03060d, 0.82).setOrigin(0).setInteractive();
      layer.add(dim);
      layer.add(
        this.add
          .text(W / 2, 470, upper(t('game.chestTitle')), {
            fontFamily: FONT,
            fontSize: '40px',
            color: PALETTE.accent,
            fontStyle: 'bold',
            stroke: '#05070f',
            strokeThickness: 8,
          })
          .setOrigin(0.5)
      );

      let chosen = false;
      const chests = values.map((v, i) => {
        const x = W / 2 + (i - 1) * 200;
        const y = 700;
        const box = this.add.container(x, y);
        const glow = this.add
          .image(0, -10, 'dot')
          .setDisplaySize(200, 200)
          .setTint(0xffd166)
          .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD)
          .setAlpha(0);
        const body = this.add.graphics();
        body.fillStyle(0x000000, 0.35);
        body.fillEllipse(0, 66, 150, 24);
        body.fillStyle(0x6e4420, 1);
        body.fillRoundedRect(-62, -6, 124, 70, 10);
        body.fillStyle(0x8a5a2b, 1);
        body.fillRoundedRect(-62, -6, 124, 54, 10);
        body.fillStyle(0xffd166, 1);
        body.fillRect(-62, 16, 124, 8);
        const lid = this.add.graphics();
        lid.fillStyle(0xa8702f, 1);
        lid.fillRoundedRect(-66, -46, 132, 46, { tl: 26, tr: 26, bl: 6, br: 6 });
        lid.fillStyle(0xffd166, 1);
        lid.fillRect(-66, -8, 132, 8);
        lid.fillRoundedRect(-12, -10, 24, 28, 6);
        const valText = this.add
          .text(0, -84, `+${v} XP`, {
            fontFamily: FONT,
            fontSize: '34px',
            color: PALETTE.accent,
            fontStyle: 'bold',
            stroke: '#05070f',
            strokeThickness: 6,
          })
          .setOrigin(0.5)
          .setAlpha(0);
        box.add([glow, body, lid, valText]);
        layer.add(box);
        this.tweens.add({
          targets: box,
          y: y - 10,
          duration: 700 + i * 130,
          yoyo: true,
          repeat: -1,
          ease: 'Sine.easeInOut',
        });
        const zone = this.add.zone(x, y, 160, 140).setOrigin(0.5).setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
        layer.add(zone);
        return { box, lid, glow, valText, zone, v, x, y };
      });

      const reveal = (idx: number) => {
        if (chosen) return;
        chosen = true;
        chests.forEach((c, i) => {
          const mine = i === idx;
          this.tweens.killTweensOf(c.box);
          this.tweens.add({ targets: c.box, y: c.y, duration: 120 });
          this.tweens.add({
            targets: c.lid,
            y: -44,
            angle: -18,
            duration: 340,
            delay: mine ? 0 : 280,
            ease: 'Back.easeOut',
          });
          this.tweens.add({
            targets: [c.valText, c.glow],
            alpha: mine ? 1 : 0.4,
            duration: 300,
            delay: mine ? 120 : 420,
          });
          if (mine) this.tweens.add({ targets: c.valText, scale: 1.35, duration: 300, delay: 120, ease: 'Back.easeOut' });
        });
        const c = chests[idx];
        play('treasure');
        haptic('success');
        this.confetti.emitParticleAt(c.x, c.y - 20, 60);
        this.burst.emitParticleAt(c.x, c.y - 20, 36);
        this.shockwave(c.x, c.y - 20, 0xffd166);
        this.time.delayedCall(1800, () => {
          layer.destroy();
          resolve(c.v);
        });
      };

      chests.forEach((c, i) =>
        c.zone.on('pointerdown', () => {
          unlockAudio();
          reveal(i);
        })
      );
      this.time.delayedCall(9000, () => reveal(1));
    });
  }

  // ================= ROZETLER =================

  private checkBadges(): void {
    const ids = newAchievements(this.meta);
    if (ids.length) {
      this.meta.unlocked.push(...ids);
      this.toastQueue.push(...ids.map((id) => `ach:${id}`));
      this.flushToast();
    }
    saveMeta(this.meta);
  }

  private flushToast(): void {
    if (this.toasting) return;
    const item = this.toastQueue.shift();
    if (!item) return;
    const [kind, id] = item.split(':');
    this.toasting = true;
    play('badge');

    const box = this.add.container(W / 2, -70).setDepth(230);
    const bg = this.add.graphics();
    bg.fillStyle(0x14223f, 0.97);
    bg.fillRoundedRect(-270, -40, 540, 80, 24);
    bg.lineStyle(2, 0xffd166, 1);
    bg.strokeRoundedRect(-270, -40, 540, 80, 24);
    const icon = this.add.image(-222, 0, 'star5').setDisplaySize(46, 46).setTint(0xffd166);
    const head = this.add
      .text(-186, -22, upper(t(kind === 'mis' ? 'game.mission' : 'game.badge')), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '14px',
        color: PALETTE.accent2,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setLetterSpacing(2);
    const name = this.add.text(-186, -2, t(kind === 'mis' ? `mis.${id}` : `ach.${id}`), {
      fontFamily: FONT,
      fontSize: '26px',
      color: PALETTE.text,
      fontStyle: 'bold',
    });
    box.add([bg, icon, head, name]);
    this.burst?.emitParticleAt(W / 2 - 222, 270, 14);

    this.tweens.add({ targets: box, y: 268, duration: 380, ease: 'Back.easeOut' });
    this.tweens.add({
      targets: box,
      y: -90,
      duration: 320,
      delay: 2400,
      ease: 'Cubic.easeIn',
      onComplete: () => {
        box.destroy();
        this.toasting = false;
        this.flushToast();
      },
    });
  }

  private clearPath(): void {
    this.path = [];
    this.refreshPath();
  }

  private refreshPath(): void {
    this.pathG.clear();
    if (this.path.length >= 2) {
      // Tek renk, net kırmızı ip — seçilen peteklerin üstünde, harflerin altında.
      this.pathG.lineStyle(7, 0xff1744, 1);
      this.pathG.beginPath();
      const first = this.posOf(this.path[0]);
      this.pathG.moveTo(this.originX + first.x, this.originY + first.y);
      for (let k = 1; k < this.path.length; k++) {
        const p = this.posOf(this.path[k]);
        this.pathG.lineTo(this.originX + p.x, this.originY + p.y);
      }
      this.pathG.strokePath();
      // Bağlantı noktaları da kırmızı olsun ki yön daha net görünsün.
      this.pathG.fillStyle(0xff1744, 1);
      for (const i of this.path) {
        const p = this.posOf(i);
        this.pathG.fillCircle(this.originX + p.x, this.originY + p.y, 5);
      }
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < this.hexViews.length; i++) {
      const on = this.path.includes(i);
      if (on === !!this.hexOnFlags[i]) continue;
      this.hexOnFlags[i] = on;

      const p = this.posOf(i);
      const baseX = this.originX + p.x;
      const baseY = this.originY + p.y;
      const img = this.hexViews[i];
      const letter = this.letterTexts[i];
      const glow = this.hexGlows[i];

      const shadow = this.hexShadows[i];
      this.tweens.killTweensOf([img, letter, glow, shadow]);

      if (on) {
        // Petek yerinden yükselir, altında gölge büyür: gerçek 3B basma hissi.
        img.setTexture('hexOn');
        this.ring(baseX, baseY, 0xffd166);
        img.setPosition(baseX, baseY + 5).setScale(0.9);
        this.tweens.add({ targets: img, y: baseY - 16, scale: 1.1, duration: 240, ease: 'Back.easeOut' });
        this.tweens.add({ targets: glow, alpha: 0.8, duration: 170 });
        this.tweens.add({
          targets: shadow,
          alpha: 0.55,
          scale: 1.18,
          x: baseX + 6,
          y: baseY + 14,
          duration: 220,
          ease: 'Sine.easeOut',
        });
        letter.setColor(PALETTE.hexTextOn).setScale(0.92).setY(baseY + 2);
        this.tweens.add({ targets: letter, y: baseY - 20, scale: 1.3, duration: 250, ease: 'Back.easeOut' });
      } else {
        img.setTexture(this.baseTex(i));
        this.tweens.add({ targets: img, y: baseY, scale: 1, duration: 200, ease: 'Bounce.easeOut' });
        this.tweens.add({ targets: glow, alpha: 0, duration: 140 });
        this.tweens.add({
          targets: shadow,
          alpha: 0.3,
          scale: 1,
          x: baseX,
          y: baseY + 9,
          duration: 200,
          ease: 'Sine.easeOut',
        });
        letter.setColor(PALETTE.hexText).setScale(1).setY(baseY - 3);
        this.tweens.add({ targets: letter, y: baseY - 3, scale: 1, duration: 170, ease: 'Sine.easeOut' });
      }
    }

    const w = this.path.map((i) => (this.jokers.has(i) ? '★' : this.board[i].letter)).join('');
    this.wordText.setText(w ? display(w, this.lang) : '');
  }

  private updateHud(): void {
    if (this.scoreText && this.score !== this.shownScore) {
      this.shownScore = this.score;
      this.scoreText.setText(fmtInt(this.score));
      this.tweens.add({ targets: this.scoreText, scale: { from: 1.14, to: 1 }, duration: 200 });
    }
    this.timeText?.setText(`${upper(t('common.time'))} ${this.secondsLeft}`);
    this.shownTimeColor = '';
    this.foundText?.setText(
      `${t('common.found')} ${this.found.size} ${t('common.words')}  •  ${t('game.minLength', { n: this.def.minLength })}`
    );
    this.comboText?.setText(this.combo >= 2 ? upper(t('common.combo', { n: this.combo })) : '');
    this.refreshTreasure();
    if (!this.bar) return;

    const w = 480;
    const x = W / 2 - w / 2;
    const y = 168;
    // Çubuk 0 → 2×hedef; yıldız işaretleri 1×, 1.4×, 2× noktalarında.
    const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, this.score / (this.def.target * 2)));
    this.bar.clear();
    this.bar.fillStyle(0x0c1530, 1);
    this.bar.fillRoundedRect(x, y, w, 14, 7);
    if (pct > 0) {
      const fw = Math.max(14, w * pct);
      this.bar.fillStyle(pct >= 0.5 ? 0x37e6a6 : 0xffb347, 1);
      this.bar.fillRoundedRect(x, y, fw, 14, 7);
      this.bar.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.28);
      this.bar.fillRoundedRect(x + 3, y + 2, Math.max(8, fw - 6), 4, 2);
    }
    this.bar.lineStyle(1, 0x34507f, 1);
    this.bar.strokeRoundedRect(x, y, w, 14, 7);

    const stars = starsFor(this.score, this.def.target);
    this.starMarks.forEach((m, k) => m.setTint(k < stars ? 0xffd166 : 0x24365f));
    if (stars > this.starsLit) {
      this.starsLit = stars;
      const m = this.starMarks[stars - 1];
      if (m && this.burst) {
        play('star', stars);
        this.burst.emitParticleAt(m.x, m.y, 18);
        this.tweens.add({ targets: m, scale: { from: 0.9, to: 24 / 64 }, duration: 420, ease: 'Back.easeOut' });
      }
    }
  }

  private leaveLevel(): void {
    if (this.ready && !this.ended) {
      this.bridge.track('quit', {
        level: this.level,
        score: this.score,
        found: this.found.size,
        secondsLeft: this.secondsLeft,
      });
    }
    this.ended = true;
    this.timerEvent?.remove();
    setBackButton(null);
    this.scene.start('Menu');
  }

  // ================= BİTİŞ =================

  private async endGame(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.ended) return;
    this.ended = true;
    this.timerEvent?.remove();
    this.clearPath();
    this.bridge.track('level_start_end', { level: this.level });

    const won = this.score >= this.def.target;
    const stars = starsFor(this.score, this.def.target);
    let xp = xpFor(this.level, this.score, stars);

    // Günlük seri + günlük meydan okuma + rozetler
    const today = dayKey(Date.now());
    const day = touchDay(this.meta, today);
    this.meta.stats.plays += 1;
    if (won) this.meta.stats.wins += 1;
    const notes: string[] = [];
    if (this.meta.streak > 0) notes.push(t('game.streak', { n: this.meta.streak }));
    if (day.isNewDay) {
      const bonusXp = streakBonusXp(this.meta.streak);
      xp += bonusXp;
      notes.push(t('game.streakXp', { n: bonusXp }));
    }
    if (this.daily && won && !dailyDone(this.meta, today)) {
      this.meta.dailyDay = today;
      this.meta.dailyScore = this.score;
      xp += DAILY_BONUS_XP;
      notes.push(t('game.dailyDone', { n: DAILY_BONUS_XP }));
    }
    const missionXp = takePendingXp(this.meta);
    if (missionXp > 0) {
      xp += missionXp;
      notes.push(t('game.missionXp', { n: missionXp }));
    }
    this.resultNote = notes.join('   •   ');
    this.checkBadges();
    play(won ? 'win' : 'lose');
    haptic(won ? 'success' : 'error');
    this.cameras.main.flash(won ? 220 : 160, won ? 255 : 120, won ? 220 : 40, won ? 150 : 40);

    // Kazanınca sandık seçimi: ekstra XP.
    if (won) {
      const chestXp = await this.pickChest(stars);
      xp += chestXp;
      notes.push(t('game.chestWin', { n: chestXp }));
      this.resultNote = notes.join('   •   ');
    }

    let xpGained = xp;
    try {
      const outcome = await this.bridge.finish({
        score: this.score,
        durationMs: Date.now() - this.startedAt,
        // Günlük meydan okuma normal bölüm ilerlemesini etkilemez.
        level: this.daily ? 0 : won ? this.level : Math.max(0, this.level - 1),
        xp,
        stats: {
          stars,
          found: this.found.size,
          maxCombo: this.maxCombo,
          hints: this.hintCount,
          daily: this.daily ? 1 : 0,
          streak: this.meta.streak,
          target: this.def.target,
        },
        clientVersion: '0.1.0',
      });
      xpGained = outcome.xpGained;
    } catch {
      /* bağlantı yoksa yerel sonuç */
    }

    this.showResult(won, stars, xpGained);
  }

  private showResult(won: boolean, stars: number, xpGained: number): void {
    const profile = loadProfile();
    const info = levelInfo(profile.xp);
    const best = profile.games['word-hunt']?.bestScore ?? this.score;

    const layer = this.add.container(0, 0).setDepth(200);
    const dim = this.add
      .rectangle(0, 0, W, H, 0x03060d, 0.84)
      .setOrigin(0)
      .setInteractive();
    layer.add(dim);

    const panelY = 380;
    const panel = this.add.graphics();
    panel.fillStyle(0x101b30, 1);
    panel.fillRoundedRect(70, panelY, W - 140, 520, 30);
    panel.lineStyle(2, won ? 0x2f7a5f : 0x4a2130, 1);
    panel.strokeRoundedRect(70, panelY, W - 140, 520, 30);
    layer.add(panel);

    const title = this.add
      .text(W / 2, panelY + 58, upper(won ? t('game.won') : t('game.lost')), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '36px',
        color: won ? PALETTE.success : PALETTE.danger,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5);
    layer.add(title);

    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const x = W / 2 + (i - 1) * 96;
      const earned = i < stars;
      const star = this.add
        .image(x, panelY + 152, 'star5')
        .setDisplaySize(earned ? 72 : 56, earned ? 72 : 56)
        .setTint(earned ? 0xffd166 : 0x24365f)
        .setAlpha(earned ? 1 : 0.5);
      layer.add(star);
      if (earned) {
        star.setScale(0);
        this.tweens.add({
          targets: star,
          scale: 72 / 64,
          duration: 380,
          delay: 220 + i * 160,
          ease: 'Back.easeOut',
        });
      }
    }

    if (this.resultNote) {
      layer.add(
        this.add
          .text(W / 2, panelY + 188, this.resultNote, {
            fontFamily: FONT,
            fontSize: '15px',
            color: PALETTE.accent2,
            fontStyle: 'bold',
            align: 'center',
            wordWrap: { width: 500 },
          })
          .setOrigin(0.5, 0)
      );
    }

    const lines: Array<[string, string, string]> = [
      [upper(t('common.score')), fmtInt(this.score), PALETTE.text],
      [upper(t('common.target')), fmtInt(this.def.target), PALETTE.textDim],
      [upper(t('common.best')), fmtInt(best), PALETTE.accent],
      [upper(t('common.found')), `${this.found.size} ${t('common.words')}`, PALETTE.textDim],
      [upper(t('common.xpEarned')), `+${xpGained}`, PALETTE.success],
    ];
    lines.forEach(([label, value, color], i) => {
      const y = panelY + 244 + i * 42;
      layer.add(
        this.add
          .text(110, y, label, {
            fontFamily: FONT,
            fontSize: '18px',
            color: PALETTE.textDim,
            fontStyle: 'bold',
          })
          .setOrigin(0, 0.5)
      );
      layer.add(
        this.add
          .text(W - 110, y, value, {
            fontFamily: FONT,
            fontSize: '22px',
            color,
            fontStyle: 'bold',
          })
          .setOrigin(1, 0.5)
      );
    });

    const buttons: Array<{ label: string; primary?: boolean; action: () => void }> = [
      { label: upper(t('common.menu')), action: () => this.scene.start('Menu') },
      { label: upper(t('common.retry')), action: () => this.scene.restart({ level: this.level, daily: this.daily }) },
    ];
    if (won && !this.daily && this.level < MAX_LEVEL) {
      buttons.push({
        label: upper(t('common.next')),
        primary: true,
        action: () => this.scene.restart({ level: this.level + 1 }),
      });
    } else {
      buttons[1].primary = true;
    }

    const totalW = W - 200;
    const gap = 14;
    const bw = (totalW - gap * (buttons.length - 1)) / buttons.length;
    buttons.forEach((b, i) => {
      const bx = 100 + i * (bw + gap) + bw / 2;
      addButton(
        this,
        bx,
        panelY + 470,
        bw,
        64,
        b.label,
        { primary: b.primary, fontSize: 21, pulse: b.primary },
        b.action,
        layer
      );
    });

    layer.add(
      this.add
        .text(W / 2, panelY + 505, t('common.levelOf', { level: info.level, title: info.title }), {
          fontFamily: FONT,
          fontSize: '16px',
          color: PALETTE.textDim,
        })
        .setOrigin(0.5)
    );

    layer.setAlpha(0);
    this.tweens.add({ targets: layer, alpha: 1, duration: 260 });
    if (won) this.celebrate();
  }
}


##############################################################################
# DOSYA: games/word-hunt/src/scenes/MenuScene.ts
##############################################################################
import Phaser from 'phaser';
import { PALETTE, W, H, HEX_SIZE } from '../theme.js';
import { levelDef, MAX_LEVEL } from '../core/levels.js';
import {
  fmtInt,
  getLang,
  isMuted,
  levelInfo,
  loadProfile,
  play,
  setMuted,
  t,
  toggleLang,
  unlockAudio,
  upper,
} from '../../../shared/game-kit/src/index.js';
import { closeApp, haptic, setBackButton, tgApp } from '../telegram.js';
import {
  activeStreak,
  DAILY_BONUS_XP,
  dailyDone,
  dailyLevel,
  dayKey,
  loadMeta,
  MISSIONS,
  missionsFor,
} from '../core/meta.js';
import { addButton } from '../ui.js';

const FONT = '"Trebuchet MS", "Segoe UI", system-ui, sans-serif';

export class MenuScene extends Phaser.Scene {
  private level = 1;
  private requestedLevel = 0;
  private levelText!: Phaser.GameObjects.Text;
  private targetText!: Phaser.GameObjects.Text;
  private mutedLabel!: Phaser.GameObjects.Text;

  constructor() {
    super('Menu');
  }

  init(data: { level?: number }): void {
    this.requestedLevel = Math.max(0, Number(data?.level) || 0);
  }

  create(): void {
    this.level = this.requestedLevel || this.nextLevel();
    this.buildBackground();
    this.buildTitle();
    this.buildLevelCard();
    this.buildPlayButton();
    this.buildExtras();
    this.buildProfileBar();
    this.buildSoundToggle();
    this.buildLangToggle();

    setBackButton(() => {
      haptic('light');
      closeApp();
    });

    this.add
      .text(W / 2, H - 34, t('menu.footer'), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '16px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setAlpha(0.7);

    this.cameras.main.fadeIn(320, 7, 11, 24);
  }

  private nextLevel(): number {
    const p = loadProfile();
    const best = p.games['word-hunt']?.bestLevel ?? 0;
    return Math.max(1, Math.min(MAX_LEVEL, best + 1));
  }

  private buildBackground(): void {
    const g = this.add.graphics();
    g.fillGradientStyle(0x0d1730, 0x0d1730, 0x05070f, 0x05070f, 1);
    g.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    this.add
      .image(W / 2, 320, 'dot')
      .setDisplaySize(900, 900)
      .setAlpha(0.07)
      .setTint(0xffd166)
      .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD);

    // Dekoratif petek
    for (let c = 0; c < 5; c++) {
      for (let r = 0; r < 4; r++) {
        const x = 70 + c * HEX_SIZE * 1.5;
        const y = 1090 + Math.sqrt(3) * HEX_SIZE * (r + 0.5 * (c & 1));
        const hex = this.add
          .image(x, y, 'hex')
          .setDisplaySize(HEX_SIZE * 1.5, HEX_SIZE * 1.5)
          .setAlpha(0.18);
        this.tweens.add({
          targets: hex,
          alpha: 0.32,
          duration: Phaser.Math.Between(1600, 2800),
          yoyo: true,
          repeat: -1,
          delay: (c + r) * 90,
        });
      }
    }
  }

  private buildTitle(): void {
    const mainTitle = getLang() === 'tr' ? 'KELIME AVI' : 'WORD HUNT';
    const subTitle = getLang() === 'tr' ? 'WORD HUNT' : 'KELIME AVI';
    const title = this.add
      .text(W / 2, 250, mainTitle, {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '66px',
        color: PALETTE.accent,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5);
    this.add
      .text(W / 2, 253, mainTitle, {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '66px',
        color: '#8a5a10',
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setAlpha(0.5);

    this.tweens.add({
      targets: title,
      y: '-=7',
      duration: 2000,
      yoyo: true,
      repeat: -1,
      ease: 'Sine.easeInOut',
    });

    this.add
      .text(W / 2, 318, subTitle, {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '16px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setLetterSpacing(4);

    this.add
      .text(W / 2, 356, t('menu.tagline'), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '16px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setLetterSpacing(1);
  }

  private buildLevelCard(): void {
    const g = this.add.graphics();
    g.fillStyle(0x13203a, 0.95);
    g.fillRoundedRect(W / 2 - 270, 410, 540, 190, 26);
    g.lineStyle(2, 0x2a3c60, 1);
    g.strokeRoundedRect(W / 2 - 270, 410, 540, 190, 26);

    this.add
      .text(W / 2, 444, upper(t('menu.levelLabel')), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '17px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setLetterSpacing(3);

    this.levelText = this.add
      .text(W / 2, 510, String(this.level), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '64px',
        color: PALETTE.text,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5);

    this.targetText = this.add
      .text(W / 2, 566, '', {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '18px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5);

    const chevron = (x: number, label: string, dir: number) => {
      const tx = this.add
        .text(x, 510, label, {
          fontFamily: FONT,
          fontSize: '54px',
          color: PALETTE.accent,
          fontStyle: 'bold',
        })
        .setOrigin(0.5)
        .setAlpha(0.85)
        .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
      tx.on('pointerdown', () => {
        unlockAudio();
        play('click');
        const next = Phaser.Math.Clamp(this.level + dir, 1, this.nextLevel());
        if (next !== this.level) {
          this.level = next;
          this.refreshLevel();
        }
      });
    };
    chevron(W / 2 - 210, '‹', -1);
    chevron(W / 2 + 210, '›', 1);
    this.refreshLevel();
  }

  private refreshLevel(): void {
    const def = levelDef(this.level);
    this.levelText.setText(String(def.level));
    this.targetText.setText(t('menu.target', { score: fmtInt(def.target), seconds: def.seconds }));
    this.tweens.add({
      targets: this.levelText,
      scale: { from: 1.18, to: 1 },
      duration: 220,
      ease: 'Back.easeOut',
    });
  }

  private buildPlayButton(): void {
    const btn = addButton(
      this,
      W / 2,
      710,
      440,
      116,
      upper(t('common.play')),
      { primary: true, pulse: true, fontSize: 46, letterSpacing: 4 },
      () => {
        this.cameras.main.fadeOut(220, 7, 11, 24);
        this.cameras.main.once('camerafadeoutcomplete', () => {
          this.scene.start('Game', { level: this.level });
        });
      }
    );
    this.tweens.add({
      targets: btn.container,
      y: '+=6',
      duration: 1600,
      yoyo: true,
      repeat: -1,
      ease: 'Sine.easeInOut',
    });
  }

  /** Seri rozeti ve günlük meydan okuma düğmesi: oyuncuyu her gün geri getirir. */
  private buildExtras(): void {
    const meta = loadMeta();
    const today = dayKey(Date.now());
    const streak = activeStreak(meta, today);

    if (streak > 0) {
      const g = this.add.graphics();
      g.fillStyle(0x13203a, 0.95);
      g.fillRoundedRect(W / 2 - 150, 100, 300, 46, 23);
      g.lineStyle(2, 0xff9f43, 0.9);
      g.strokeRoundedRect(W / 2 - 150, 100, 300, 46, 23);
      this.add.image(W / 2 - 116, 123, 'star5').setDisplaySize(26, 26).setTint(0xff9f43);
      this.add
        .text(W / 2 + 10, 123, t('menu.streak', { n: streak }), {
          fontFamily: FONT,
          fontSize: '20px',
          color: PALETTE.accent2,
          fontStyle: 'bold',
        })
        .setOrigin(0.5);
    }

    const done = dailyDone(meta, today);
    addButton(
      this,
      W / 2,
      826,
      440,
      80,
      upper(done ? t('menu.dailyDone') : t('menu.daily')),
      {
        sub: done ? t('menu.dailyAgain') : t('menu.dailyReward', { n: DAILY_BONUS_XP }),
        fontSize: 21,
        border: done ? 0x2a3c60 : 0xffd166,
        labelColor: done ? PALETTE.textDim : PALETTE.accent,
        pulse: !done,
      },
      () => {
        this.cameras.main.fadeOut(220, 7, 11, 24);
        this.cameras.main.once('camerafadeoutcomplete', () => {
          this.scene.start('Game', { level: dailyLevel(today), daily: true });
        });
      }
    );

    this.buildMissions(meta, today);
  }

  /** Günlük görev kartı: 3 görev, ilerleme çubuğu ve tamamlanma işareti. */
  private buildMissions(meta: ReturnType<typeof loadMeta>, today: string): void {
    const m = missionsFor(meta, today);
    const top = 884;
    const g = this.add.graphics();
    g.fillStyle(0x13203a, 0.95);
    g.fillRoundedRect(W / 2 - 210, top, 420, 104, 22);
    g.lineStyle(2, 0x2a3c60, 1);
    g.strokeRoundedRect(W / 2 - 210, top, 420, 104, 22);

    this.add
      .text(W / 2, top + 18, upper(t('menu.missions')), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '14px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5)
      .setLetterSpacing(3);

    MISSIONS.forEach((d, i) => {
      const cx = W / 2 - 140 + i * 140;
      const value = Math.min(d.goal, m[d.id]);
      const complete = m.done.includes(d.id);
      this.add
        .text(cx, top + 44, t(`mis.${d.id}`), {
          fontFamily: FONT,
          fontSize: '15px',
          color: complete ? PALETTE.success : PALETTE.text,
          fontStyle: 'bold',
        })
        .setOrigin(0.5);
      const bar = this.add.graphics();
      bar.fillStyle(0x24365f, 1);
      bar.fillRoundedRect(cx - 52, top + 62, 104, 10, 5);
      if (value > 0) {
        bar.fillStyle(complete ? 0x37e6a6 : 0xffb347, 1);
        bar.fillRoundedRect(cx - 52, top + 62, Math.max(10, (104 * value) / d.goal), 10, 5);
      }
      this.add
        .text(cx, top + 84, complete ? `✓ +${d.xp} XP` : `${value}/${d.goal}`, {
          fontFamily: FONT,
          fontSize: '14px',
          color: complete ? PALETTE.success : PALETTE.textDim,
        })
        .setOrigin(0.5);
    });
  }

  private buildProfileBar(): void {
    const p = loadProfile();
    const info = levelInfo(p.xp);
    const stats = p.games['word-hunt'];
    const barW = 460;
    const x = W / 2 - barW / 2;
    const y = 1030;

    const tgUser = tgApp()?.initDataUnsafe?.user;
    if (tgUser) {
      const name = [tgUser.first_name, tgUser.last_name].filter(Boolean).join(' ').trim() || tgUser.username;
      if (name) {
        this.add
          .text(W / 2, y - 40, t('common.welcome', { name }), {
            fontFamily: FONT,
            fontSize: '18px',
            color: PALETTE.accent,
            fontStyle: 'bold',
          })
          .setOrigin(0.5)
          .setAlpha(0.9);
      }
    }

    this.add
      .text(W / 2, y, t('common.levelOf', { level: info.level, title: info.title }), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '21px',
        color: PALETTE.text,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5);

    const track = this.add.graphics();
    track.fillStyle(0x16213f, 1);
    track.fillRoundedRect(x, y + 32, barW, 16, 8);
    if (info.progress > 0) {
      const fill = this.add.graphics();
      fill.fillStyle(0xffd166, 1);
      fill.fillRoundedRect(x, y + 32, Math.max(16, barW * info.progress), 16, 8);
      fill.setAlpha(0.9);
    }

    this.add
      .text(W / 2, y + 64, `${info.xpIntoLevel} / ${info.xpForNext} XP`, {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '16px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5);

    if (stats && stats.bestScore > 0) {
      this.add
        .text(W / 2, y + 104, `${t('common.best')}: ${fmtInt(stats.bestScore)}`, {
          fontFamily: FONT,
          fontSize: '20px',
          color: PALETTE.accent,
          fontStyle: 'bold',
        })
        .setOrigin(0.5);
    }
  }

  private buildSoundToggle(): void {
    this.mutedLabel = this.add
      .text(W - 24, 30, upper(isMuted() ? t('common.soundOff') : t('common.soundOn')), {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '15px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(1, 0)
      .setAlpha(0.85)
      .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });

    this.mutedLabel.on('pointerdown', () => {
      unlockAudio();
      const next = !isMuted();
      setMuted(next);
      this.mutedLabel.setText(upper(next ? t('common.soundOff') : t('common.soundOn')));
      if (!next) play('click');
    });
  }

  private buildLangToggle(): void {
    const y = 30;
    this.add
      .text(74, y, '/', {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: '16px',
        color: PALETTE.textDim,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5, 0)
      .setAlpha(0.6);

    const chip = (label: string, x: number) => {
      const text = this.add
        .text(x, y, label, {
          fontFamily: FONT,
          fontSize: '17px',
          color: PALETTE.textDim,
          fontStyle: 'bold',
        })
        .setOrigin(0.5, 0)
        .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
      text.on('pointerdown', () => {
        unlockAudio();
        play('click');
        if (getLang() !== label.toLowerCase()) toggleLang();
        this.scene.restart({ level: this.level });
      });
      return text;
    };

    const tr = chip('TR', 46);
    const en = chip('EN', 102);
    const active = getLang();
    tr.setColor(active === 'tr' ? PALETTE.accent : PALETTE.textDim).setAlpha(active === 'tr' ? 1 : 0.6);
    en.setColor(active === 'en' ? PALETTE.accent : PALETTE.textDim).setAlpha(active === 'en' ? 1 : 0.6);
  }
}

##############################################################################
# DOSYA: games/word-hunt/src/telegram.ts
##############################################################################
/**
 * TELEGRAM MINI APP KATMANI
 * ------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Oyun Telegram içinde açıldığında:
 *   - tam ekrana yayılır (expand)
 *   - dikey kaydırma kapanır (oyun tahtası kaymasın)
 *   - geri tuşu oyun içinde menüye döner
 *   - eşleşmelerde dokunsal geri bildirim (haptic) verilir
 *
 * Telegram dışında (tarayıcıda) açılırsa hepsi sessizce devre dışı kalır.
 */

type TgUser = {
  id?: number;
  first_name?: string;
  last_name?: string;
  username?: string;
  language_code?: string;
};
type WebApp = any;

let backHandler: (() => void) | null = null;

export function tgApp(): WebApp | null {
  return (globalThis as any).Telegram?.WebApp ?? null;
}

export function isTelegram(): boolean {
  const w = tgApp();
  return !!w && !!w.initData;
}

export interface TgInfo {
  inTelegram: boolean;
  userName: string | null;
  /** Telegram kullanıcı dili (tr, en, tr-TR...) — dil seçimi için ipucu. */
  language: string | null;
}

export function initTelegram(): TgInfo {
  const w = tgApp();
  if (!w) return { inTelegram: false, userName: null, language: null };

  try {
    w.ready();
    w.expand();
    if (typeof w.disableVerticalSwipes === 'function') w.disableVerticalSwipes();
    if (typeof w.setHeaderColor === 'function') w.setHeaderColor('#05070f');
    if (typeof w.setBackgroundColor === 'function') w.setBackgroundColor('#05070f');
  } catch {
    /* eski Bot API sürümleri */
  }

  const u: TgUser | undefined = w.initDataUnsafe?.user;
  const userName = u
    ? [u.first_name, u.last_name].filter(Boolean).join(' ').trim() || u.username || null
    : null;

  return {
    inTelegram: true,
    userName,
    language: u?.language_code ?? null,
  };
}

/** Geri tuşunu sahneye bağlar. null → gizle. */
export function setBackButton(handler: (() => void) | null): void {
  const w = tgApp();
  if (!w?.BackButton) return;
  try {
    if (backHandler) w.BackButton.offClick(backHandler);
    backHandler = handler;
    if (handler) {
      w.BackButton.onClick(handler);
      w.BackButton.show();
    } else {
      w.BackButton.hide();
    }
  } catch {
    /* yut */
  }
}

export type HapticKind = 'light' | 'medium' | 'heavy' | 'success' | 'error' | 'warning';

export function haptic(kind: HapticKind = 'light'): void {
  const h = tgApp()?.HapticFeedback;
  if (!h) return;
  try {
    if (kind === 'success' || kind === 'error' || kind === 'warning') h.notificationOccurred(kind);
    else h.impactOccurred(kind);
  } catch {
    /* yut */
  }
}

export function closeApp(): void {
  const w = tgApp();
  try {
    w?.close?.();
  } catch {
    /* yut */
  }
}

##############################################################################
# DOSYA: games/word-hunt/src/ui.ts
##############################################################################
import Phaser from 'phaser';
import { PALETTE } from './theme.js';
import { play, unlockAudio } from '../../shared/game-kit/src/index.js';
import { haptic } from './telegram.js';

const FONT = '"Trebuchet MS", "Segoe UI", system-ui, sans-serif';

export interface ButtonOpts {
  /** Altın renkli ana buton. */
  primary?: boolean;
  fontSize?: number;
  /** Etiket altında küçük açıklama. */
  sub?: string;
  /** Ana butonun etrafındaki nabız parıltısı. */
  pulse?: boolean;
  /** Kenarlık rengi (ikincil butonlar için vurgu). */
  border?: number;
  labelColor?: string;
  letterSpacing?: number;
}

export interface UiButton {
  container: Phaser.GameObjects.Container;
  label: Phaser.GameObjects.Text;
  sub?: Phaser.GameObjects.Text;
}

/**
 * Modern 3B buton: kalın alt kenar, cam parlaması, basınca çöker, bırakınca zıplar.
 * Dokunma `pointerdown` ile alınır (Telegram WebView'de en güvenilir olan bu);
 * olay yayılımı durdurulur, böylece arkadaki tahta girdisini tetiklemez.
 */
export function addButton(
  scene: Phaser.Scene,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  w: number,
  h: number,
  text: string,
  opts: ButtonOpts,
  onTap: () => void,
  parent?: Phaser.GameObjects.Container
): UiButton {
  const primary = !!opts.primary;
  const r = Math.min(28, h / 2.4);
  const lip = Math.max(5, Math.round(h * 0.09));
  const face = h - lip;

  const container = scene.add.container(x, y);

  if (opts.pulse) {
    const glow = scene.add.graphics();
    glow.fillStyle(primary ? 0xffd166 : 0x7dffe0, 0.18);
    glow.fillRoundedRect(-w / 2 - 14, -h / 2 - 12, w + 28, h + 30, r + 10);
    container.add(glow);
    scene.tweens.add({ targets: glow, alpha: 0.35, duration: 900, yoyo: true, repeat: -1, ease: 'Sine.easeInOut' });
  }

  const g = scene.add.graphics();
  g.fillStyle(0x000000, 0.32);
  g.fillRoundedRect(-w / 2 + 2, -h / 2 + lip + 4, w, face, r);
  g.fillStyle(primary ? 0xa86a12 : 0x0d1730, 1);
  g.fillRoundedRect(-w / 2, -h / 2 + lip, w, face, r);
  g.fillStyle(primary ? 0xffd166 : 0x243658, 1);
  g.fillRoundedRect(-w / 2, -h / 2, w, face, r);
  g.fillStyle(0xffffff, primary ? 0.28 : 0.1);
  g.fillRoundedRect(-w / 2 + 6, -h / 2 + 4, w - 12, face / 2 - 3, { tl: r - 5, tr: r - 5, bl: 8, br: 8 });
  g.lineStyle(2, opts.border ?? (primary ? 0xfff0b8 : 0x3b5486), 1);
  g.strokeRoundedRect(-w / 2, -h / 2, w, face, r);
  container.add(g);

  const size = opts.fontSize ?? 22;
  const hasSub = !!opts.sub;
  const label = scene.add
    .text(0, -lip / 2 - (hasSub ? size * 0.42 : 0), text, {
      fontFamily: FONT,
      fontSize: `${size}px`,
      color: opts.labelColor ?? (primary ? '#3a2500' : PALETTE.text),
      fontStyle: 'bold',
    })
    .setOrigin(0.5);
  if (opts.letterSpacing) label.setLetterSpacing(opts.letterSpacing);
  container.add(label);

  let sub: Phaser.GameObjects.Text | undefined;
  if (hasSub) {
    sub = scene.add
      .text(0, -lip / 2 + size * 0.78, opts.sub!, {
        fontFamily: FONT,
        fontSize: `${Math.max(12, Math.round(size * 0.66))}px`,
        color: primary ? '#6b4a10' : PALETTE.textDim,
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5);
    container.add(sub);
  }

  const zone = scene.add.zone(0, 0, w, h).setOrigin(0.5).setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
  container.add(zone);

  zone.on('pointerdown', (_p: Phaser.Input.Pointer, _x: number, _y: number, ev: Phaser.Types.Input.EventData) => {
    ev?.stopPropagation?.();
    unlockAudio();
    play('click');
    haptic('light');
    scene.tweens.killTweensOf(container);
    scene.tweens.add({ targets: container, scale: 0.94, duration: 70, ease: 'Sine.easeOut' });
    const release = () => {
      scene.tweens.add({ targets: container, scale: 1, duration: 220, ease: 'Back.easeOut' });
    };
    scene.input.once('pointerup', release);
    scene.time.delayedCall(90, onTap);
  });

  if (parent) parent.add(container);
  return { container, label, sub };
}

# OYUN PLATFORMU — Proje Hafızası

> Bu dosya, projeye sonradan bakan (insan veya AI) herkesin **hiçbir şey sormadan**
> devam edebilmesi için yazıldı. Kararlar, kurallar, dosya yapısı ve mevcut durum burada.
>
> Son güncelleme: 2026-11-24 · Aşama: **2 (Gem Match + Kelime Avı bağımsız oyunlar)**
>
> **Bu depo Taskonomix'ten tamamen ayrıdır.** Taskonomix kodu, veritabanı, API'si
> ve deploy'u burada yoktur. İleride istenirse iki ürün birbirine bağlanabilir;
> bağlanmadan önce de oyunlar tek başına yayınlanabilir.

---

## 1. Temel kararlar (DEĞİŞTİRİLMEZ kabul edilir)

| # | Karar | Gerekçe |
|---|---|---|
| K1 | **Oyun önce bağımsız ürün.** Başka bir uygulamaya entegrasyon sonra ve isteğe bağlı. | Entegrasyonu erken yapmak oyun tasarımını kısıtlar. |
| K2 | **Oyun kodu hiçbir platformu tanımaz.** Sadece `GameBridge` arayüzünü bilir. | Oyunu her yerde çalıştırabilmek için. |
| K3 | **Oyun XP'si gerçek çekilebilir puana otomatik dönüşmez.** | Gerçek para = ciddi hile denetimi gerekir. |
| K4 | **Phaser yalnızca aksiyon/bulmaca oyunlarında.** Quiz, kelime, çark gibi oyunlar React + DOM. | 1.5 MB Phaser'ı metin oyununa yüklemek anlamsız. |
| K5 | **Her oyun ayrı Vite build → `dist/games/<id>/`.** | Ana paket şişmesin; oyunlar tek başına açılsın. |
| K6 | **RNG tohumu (seed) oturumdan gelir ve oyun deterministiktir.** | İleride replay ile skor doğrulaması yapılabilsin. `Math.random()` YOK. |
| K7 | **Telegram `initData` oyun koduna verilmez.** | O, ana kimlik bilgisidir. |
| K8 | **Oyun verisi genel, kimliksiz API'lerden okunmaz/yazılmaz.** | Bu depoda zaten API yok; ileride özel `/api/games/*` açılacak. |
| K9 | **İlk sürümde 2 oyun.** Sonra cilalama, sonra istenirse entegrasyon. | Yarım 6 ürün yerine tam 2 ürün. |
| K10 | **YAPACAĞIMIZ HER UYGULAMA/oyun Türkçe + İngilizce olmak zorunda.** Metin koda gömülmez; `game-kit/i18n` üzerinden `t()` ile okunur. | Kullanıcı açık istedi. Sonradan dil eklemek tüm ekranları yeniden yazdırır. |

---

## 2. Mimari

```
Tarayıcı / Telegram Mini App
        │  HTTPS
        ▼
game-host/server.cjs       ← minimal statik sunucu (API yok, DB yok)
        │  /games/<id>/
        ▼
dist/games/<id>/           ← her oyunun kendi index.html + JS'i
        │
        ▼
games/shared/game-kit      ← bridge, profil, RNG, ses, storage, i18n
```

**Bridge iki modlu:** `StandaloneBridge` (şu anki mod; seed yerel, profil
localStorage) ve `TaskonomixBridge` (gelecekte; imzalı bilet + sunucu profili).
Oyun kodu iki modda da aynıdır; sadece `bridge.start()` / `bridge.finish()`
davranışı değişir.

---

## 3. Dosya yapısı

```
games/
  shared/game-kit/src/
    types.ts      GameBridge, SessionInfo, GameResult sözleşmesi
    bridge.ts     StandaloneBridge / TaskonomixBridge
    profile.ts    XP, seviye, rekor, başarımlar (rütbe adları i18n'de)
    rng.ts        mulberry32 deterministik RNG
    audio.ts      Web Audio sentezi (dosyasız ses)
    storage.ts    güvenli localStorage
    i18n.ts       TR/EN sözlük, dil algılama, t(), fmtInt(), upper()
    index.ts      tek giriş noktası

  gem-match/
    index.html            Telegram SDK + canvas kabuğu
    vite.config.ts        base=/games/gem-match/, outDir=dist/games/gem-match
    src/
      main.ts             Phaser.Game + Telegram + dil init
      strings.ts          Gem Match TR/EN sözlüğü (kendini kaydeder)
      theme.ts            renk paleti, layout sabitleri
      telegram.ts         expand, back button, haptic, kullanıcı dili
      core/board.ts       SAF mantık (Phaser yok) — test edilebilir
      core/levels.ts      seviye/hedef/yıldız/XP kuralları
      scenes/BootScene.ts görselleri kodla üretir (asset dosyası yok)
      scenes/MenuScene.ts menü, seviye seçimi, profil, TR/EN düğmesi
      scenes/GameScene.ts tahta, animasyon, kombo, sonuç

  word-hunt/
    index.html / vite.config.ts   port 5175, /games/word-hunt/
    src/
      main.ts / theme.ts / telegram.ts / strings.ts
      core/hex.ts        petek ızgara matematiği (6 komşu, odd-q)
      core/board.ts      tahta üretimi, kelime gömme, yol kontrolü
      core/words.ts      TR/EN sözlük setleri
      core/levels.ts     süre, hedef, min harf, puan
      data/words.tr.ts   25.772 Türkçe kelime
      data/words.en.ts   14.441 İngilizce kelime
      scenes/Boot/Menu/Game

game-host/
    Dockerfile            ortak host: /games/<id>/ altında tüm oyunlar
    Dockerfile.gem-match  sadece Gem Match → kendi Coolify uygulaması
    Dockerfile.word-hunt  sadece Kelime Avı → kendi Coolify uygulaması
    server.cjs            GAME_ID varsa kök dizinde tek oyun, API yok
    README.md             Coolify ve BotFather adımları

scripts/
    test-gem-match.mjs    mantık testi (300 oyun simülasyonu)
    test-word-hunt.mjs    petek/kelime testi (92 kontrol)
    ensure-esbuild.cjs    esbuild binary garantisi
```

---

## 4. Yeni oyun ekleme rehberi

1. `games/<oyun-id>/` klasörünü aç.
2. `vite.config.ts` kopyala → `base`, `outDir`, `server.port` değerlerini değiştir.
3. `index.html` kopyala → `<title>` değiştir, Telegram SDK script'ini koru.
4. `src/theme.ts` → kendi paletin.
5. `src/strings.ts` → oyunun TR/EN metinleri; `main.ts` içinde
   `import './strings.js'` ve `initLang(tgInfo.language)` (K10).
6. Saf mantık katmanını `src/core/` altına yaz (Phaser import etme!).
   Testi: `scripts/test-<oyun>.mjs`.
7. `createBridge('<oyun-id>')` ile başla; `bridge.start()` → `seed` al.
8. Tüm rastgeleliği `createRng(seed)` üzerinden yap. `Math.random()` kullanma.
9. Oyun bitince `bridge.finish({ score, durationMs, xp, stats, clientVersion })`.
10. `package.json` içindeki `build:games` komutuna yeni build satırını ekle:
    ```
    vite build --config games/gem-match/vite.config.ts &&
    vite build --config games/<oyun-id>/vite.config.ts
    ```
11. `npm run typecheck && npm test && npm run build` → doğrula.
12. TR ve EN ekranlarını `?lang=tr` / `?lang=en` ile kontrol et.

**Kural:** Oyun içinde `localStorage`, `fetch`, `Telegram`, platform kodu doğrudan
kullanılmaz. Hepsi `game-kit` üzerinden geçer. Tek istisna: `telegram.ts`
(sadece UI: expand, geri tuşu, haptic).

### Dil kuralı (K10)

```ts
// src/strings.ts
export const STRINGS: Dict = {
  'menu.play': ['OYNA', 'PLAY'],
  'menu.target': ['Hedef {score} puan', 'Target {score} pts'],
};
registerStrings(STRINGS);
export { t, fmtInt, upper, toggleLang } from '../../shared/game-kit/src/i18n.js';

// sahne
t('menu.play');                         // OYNA | PLAY
t('menu.target', { score: fmtInt(1500) });
```

Dil sırası: `?lang=en` (test) → kullanıcı seçimi (`tq_lang_v1`) → Telegram dili →
tarayıcı dili → Türkçe. Sayılar `fmtInt()` ile dile göre biçimlenir.

---

## 5. Gem Match — teknik özet

| Konu | Detay |
|---|---|
| Tahta | 8×8, 5–6 renk |
| Eşleşme | 3+ yatay/dikey; L/T birleştirme |
| Özel taşlar | 4'lü → satır/sütun, L/T → bomba (3×3), 5'li → gökkuşağı |
| Zincirleme | Sınırsız (guard 40), kombo çarpanı `1 + (n-1)*0.5` |
| Süre | Hamle bazlı (26 → 20) |
| Yıldız | hedef ×1 = 1★, ×1.5 = 2★, ×2 = 3★ |
| XP | `6 + seviye*2 + yıldız*7 + skor/1200*3`, max 150 |
| Görseller | Kodla üretilir (`BootScene`) — asset dosyası yok |
| Ses | Web Audio sentezi — dosya yok |
| Determinizm | Aynı seed + aynı hamleler = aynı sonuç |

---

## 5b. Kelime Avı — teknik özet

| Konu | Detay |
|---|---|
| Izgara | 7×8 petek (flat-top hex, odd-q), 6 komşu yön |
| Girdi | Parmakla komşu harflere doğru çizgi; geri dönerek silinebilir |
| Kelime | En az 3 / 4 / 5 harf (seviyeye göre), sözlükte olmalı, tekrar edilemez |
| Sözlük | TR 25.772 kelime (mythes-tr), EN 14.441 kelime (google-20k) |
| Tahta | Rastgele harfler + 7-16 kelime komşu zincir olarak gömülür; seed deterministik |
| Puan | `harf² × 10 × kombo × seviye` |
| Süre | 100 saniyeden başlar, 55 saniyeye kadar düşer |
| Dil | TR/EN; tahta harfleri ve sözlük dile göre değişir |

İleride skor doğrulaması / liderlik istenirse bu depoda ayrı bir API katmanı açılır:

| Tablo | Not |
|---|---|

| `game_events` | telemetri (ilk günden gerekli) |
| `game_profiles` | `user_id, xp, best jsonb` |
| `game_xp_transactions` | `UNIQUE(session_id)` → mükerrer XP engeli |

Güvenlik: XP sunucuda hesaplanır, bilet tek kullanımlık ve süreli olur.
